`stl-utility/src/stl_shared_ptr.cpp`:

```cpp
// MSVC std::shared_ptr<T>/std::weak_ptr<T> descriptor. Layout (x64,
// VS2019/2022 ABI), per microsoft/STL's memory header:
//
//   struct shared_ptr<T> {           // sizeof == 0x10
//     T *_Ptr;                       // offset 0x00 -- raw pointer to managed object
//     _Ref_count_base *_Rep;         // offset 0x08 -- control block pointer
//   };
//   // weak_ptr<T> has the identical {_Ptr, _Rep} layout.
//
//   struct _Ref_count_base {         // control block header, stable across
//     void **vfptr;                  // offset 0x00    make_shared/new-T flavors
//     long _Uses;                    // offset 0x08 -- strong refcount
//     long _Weaks;                   // offset 0x0C -- weak refcount
//   };
//
// The element type T isn't needed to read refcounts (decode_preview only
// reads the control block's raw bytes at fixed offsets), but it IS needed
// for the applied _Ptr field to be useful for navigation/further typing in
// Hex-Rays, so it's recovered the same way stl_vector.cpp/stl_unique_ptr.cpp
// do: existing type info first, then the demangled name hint, then prompt.

#include "stl_registry.h"
#include "stl_memory.h"
#include "stl_util.h"

#include <kernwin.hpp>
#include <typeinf.hpp>

#include <cctype>
#include <cstdint>
#include <cstring>
#include <string>

namespace stl_utility
{

namespace
{

constexpr size_t STRUCT_SIZE = 0x10;
constexpr size_t PTR_OFFSET = 0x00;
constexpr size_t REP_OFFSET = 0x08;
constexpr size_t USES_OFFSET = 0x08;
constexpr size_t WEAKS_OFFSET = 0x0C;
constexpr size_t CONTROL_BLOCK_READ_SIZE = 0x10;

struct ptr_kind_t
{
  bool is_weak;
  const char *key;
  const char *display_name;
  const char *marker;            // "shared_ptr<" / "weak_ptr<"
  const char *synthetic_prefix;  // "__stl_shared_ptr_of_" / "__stl_weak_ptr_of_"
};

const ptr_kind_t SHARED_KIND =
  { false, "std_shared_ptr", "std::shared_ptr<T>", "shared_ptr<", "__stl_shared_ptr_of_" };
const ptr_kind_t WEAK_KIND =
  { true, "std_weak_ptr", "std::weak_ptr<T>", "weak_ptr<", "__stl_weak_ptr_of_" };

std::string extract_element_type_name(const ptr_kind_t &kind, const qstring *hint)
{
  if ( hint == nullptr )
    return {};

  const std::string text(hint->c_str());
  const size_t marker = text.find(kind.marker);
  if ( marker == std::string::npos )
    return {};

  const size_t args_start = marker + std::strlen(kind.marker);
  int depth = 1;
  size_t cursor = args_start;
  size_t first_comma = std::string::npos;
  for ( ; cursor < text.size() && depth > 0; ++cursor )
  {
    if ( text[cursor] == '<' )
      ++depth;
    else if ( text[cursor] == '>' )
      --depth;
    else if ( text[cursor] == ',' && depth == 1 && first_comma == std::string::npos )
      first_comma = cursor;
  }
  if ( depth != 0 )
    return {};

  const size_t args_end = first_comma != std::string::npos ? first_comma : cursor - 1;
  if ( args_end <= args_start )
    return {};

  std::string element = text.substr(args_start, args_end - args_start);
  size_t begin = 0;
  size_t end = element.size();
  while ( begin < end && std::isspace(static_cast<unsigned char>(element[begin])) != 0 )
    ++begin;
  while ( end > begin && std::isspace(static_cast<unsigned char>(element[end - 1])) != 0 )
    --end;
  return element.substr(begin, end - begin);
}

bool resolve_element_type(
      const ptr_kind_t &kind,
      const tinfo_t *existing_type,
      const qstring *demangled_name_hint,
      tinfo_t *out_element,
      qstring *out_element_name)
{
  // 1. If IDA already knows a UDT shaped like {T *_Ptr; ...}, recover T
  //    from that member's pointee.
  if ( existing_type != nullptr && !existing_type->empty() && existing_type->is_udt() )
  {
    udt_type_data_t udt;
    if ( existing_type->get_udt_details(&udt) && !udt.empty() && udt[0].type.is_ptr() )
    {
      tinfo_t pointee = udt[0].type.get_pointed_object();
      if ( !pointee.empty() )
      {
        *out_element = pointee;
        *out_element_name = printable_type_name(pointee);
        return true;
      }
    }
  }

  // 2. Parse "shared_ptr<T,...>"/"weak_ptr<T,...>" from the demangled hint.
  const std::string extracted = extract_element_type_name(kind, demangled_name_hint);
  if ( !extracted.empty() && parse_type_string(extracted.c_str(), out_element) )
  {
    *out_element_name = extracted.c_str();
    return true;
  }

  // 3. Prompt the user for the element type.
  qstring typed;
  if ( ask_str(&typed, HIST_TYPE, "%s element type (e.g. \"int\", \"MyStruct\"):", kind.display_name)
    && parse_type_string(typed.c_str(), out_element) )
  {
    *out_element_name = typed;
    return true;
  }

  return false;
}

// Registers a small documentation-only Local Type for the shared control
// block (not applied to any variable -- decode_preview reads its bytes
// directly), so users can inspect the layout in Local Types if curious.
void ensure_control_block_type_registered()
{
  tinfo_t existing;
  if ( existing.get_named_type(nullptr, "__stl_ref_count_base", BTF_STRUCT) )
    return;

  tinfo_t void_type, void_ptr, void_ptr_ptr, long_type;
  if ( !void_type.create_simple_type(BTF_VOID) || !void_ptr.create_ptr(void_type)
    || !void_ptr_ptr.create_ptr(void_ptr) || !long_type.create_simple_type(BTF_INT32) )
  {
    return;
  }

  udt_type_data_t p;
  p.is_union = false;
  p.add_member("vfptr", void_ptr_ptr, 0);
  p.add_member("_Uses", long_type, USES_OFFSET * 8);
  p.add_member("_Weaks", long_type, WEAKS_OFFSET * 8);

  tinfo_t built;
  if ( built.create_udt(p) )
    built.set_named_type(nullptr, "__stl_ref_count_base", NTF_REPLACE);
}

bool build_or_get_type_impl(
      const ptr_kind_t &kind,
      const tinfo_t *existing_type,
      const qstring *demangled_name_hint,
      tinfo_t *out_type,
      qstring *out_name)
{
  tinfo_t element;
  qstring element_name;
  if ( !resolve_element_type(kind, existing_type, demangled_name_hint, &element, &element_name) )
  {
    debug_log("stl_shared_ptr: could not resolve element type, aborting retype");
    return false;
  }

  ensure_control_block_type_registered();

  tinfo_t rep_ptr, elem_ptr;
  {
    tinfo_t void_type;
    if ( !void_type.create_simple_type(BTF_VOID) || !rep_ptr.create_ptr(void_type) )
      return false;
  }
  if ( !elem_ptr.create_ptr(element) )
    return false;

  udt_type_data_t p;
  p.is_union = false;
  p.add_member("_Ptr", elem_ptr, PTR_OFFSET * 8);
  p.add_member("_Rep", rep_ptr, REP_OFFSET * 8);

  tinfo_t built;
  if ( !built.create_udt(p) )
    return false;

  qstring synthetic_name = kind.synthetic_prefix;
  for ( size_t i = 0; i < element_name.length(); ++i )
  {
    const char c = element_name[i];
    synthetic_name.append(std::isalnum(static_cast<unsigned char>(c)) != 0 ? c : '_');
  }

  debug_log("stl_shared_ptr: building synthetic type '%s'", synthetic_name.c_str());
  if ( built.set_named_type(nullptr, synthetic_name.c_str(), NTF_REPLACE) != TERR_OK )
    return false;

  tinfo_t saved;
  if ( saved.get_named_type(nullptr, synthetic_name.c_str(), BTF_STRUCT) && saved.get_size() == STRUCT_SIZE )
    *out_type = saved;
  else
    *out_type = built;
  *out_name = synthetic_name;
  return true;
}

match_confidence_t matches_impl(
      const ptr_kind_t &kind,
      const tinfo_t *existing_type,
      const qstring *demangled_name_hint)
{
  if ( demangled_name_hint != nullptr )
  {
    const bool has_shared = demangled_name_hint->find("shared_ptr<") != qstring::npos;
    const bool has_weak = demangled_name_hint->find("weak_ptr<") != qstring::npos;
    if ( has_shared || has_weak )
      return has_weak == kind.is_weak ? MATCH_HIGH : MATCH_NONE;

    // Recognize our own previously-applied synthetic "__stl_shared_ptr_of_*"
    // / "__stl_weak_ptr_of_*" type by name, so the preview keeps rendering
    // on later decompiles without re-running the retype action.
    if ( demangled_name_hint->find(kind.synthetic_prefix) == 0 )
      return MATCH_HIGH;
  }

  if ( existing_type != nullptr && !existing_type->empty() && existing_type->get_size() == STRUCT_SIZE )
    return MATCH_AMBIGUOUS;

  return MATCH_NONE;
}

stl_decode_result_t decode_preview_impl(const ptr_kind_t &kind, ea_t instance_ea, const tinfo_t &)
{
  stl_decode_result_t result;

  if ( instance_ea == BADADDR )
  {
    result.ok = true;
    result.preview = "(no fixed address)";
    return result;
  }

  const stl_read_result_t header = stl_read_memory(instance_ea, STRUCT_SIZE);
  if ( !header.ok )
  {
    result.ok = true;
    result.preview = looks_like_stack_address(instance_ea)
      ? qstring("(stack-local, attach debugger to preview)")
      : qstring("(unable to read memory)");
    return result;
  }

  ea_t rep = BADADDR;
  std::memcpy(&rep, header.data.begin() + REP_OFFSET, sizeof(ea_t));
  result.from_live_memory = header.from_debugger;

  if ( rep == BADADDR || rep == 0 )
  {
    result.ok = true;
    result.preview = kind.is_weak ? "expired/empty" : "empty";
    return result;
  }

  const stl_read_result_t control = stl_read_memory(rep, CONTROL_BLOCK_READ_SIZE);
  if ( !control.ok )
  {
    result.ok = true;
    result.preview.sprnt("rep=%a (heap, attach debugger to preview)", rep);
    return result;
  }

  int32_t uses = 0;
  int32_t weaks = 0;
  std::memcpy(&uses, control.data.begin() + USES_OFFSET, sizeof(uses));
  std::memcpy(&weaks, control.data.begin() + WEAKS_OFFSET, sizeof(weaks));
  result.from_live_memory = result.from_live_memory || control.from_debugger;
  result.ok = true;
  result.preview.sprnt(
    "%sstrong=%d weak=%d%s",
    kind.is_weak ? "weak_ptr: " : "",
    int(uses),
    int(weaks),
    kind.is_weak && uses <= 0 ? " (expired)" : "");
  return result;
}

match_confidence_t matches_shared(const tinfo_t *t, const qstring *hint) { return matches_impl(SHARED_KIND, t, hint); }
match_confidence_t matches_weak(const tinfo_t *t, const qstring *hint) { return matches_impl(WEAK_KIND, t, hint); }
bool build_shared(til_t *, const tinfo_t *t, const qstring *hint, tinfo_t *out_type, qstring *out_name) { return build_or_get_type_impl(SHARED_KIND, t, hint, out_type, out_name); }
bool build_weak(til_t *, const tinfo_t *t, const qstring *hint, tinfo_t *out_type, qstring *out_name) { return build_or_get_type_impl(WEAK_KIND, t, hint, out_type, out_name); }
stl_decode_result_t decode_shared(ea_t ea, const tinfo_t &t) { return decode_preview_impl(SHARED_KIND, ea, t); }
stl_decode_result_t decode_weak(ea_t ea, const tinfo_t &t) { return decode_preview_impl(WEAK_KIND, ea, t); }

const stl_type_descriptor_t SHARED_DESCRIPTOR =
{
  SHARED_KIND.key, SHARED_KIND.display_name,
  &matches_shared, &build_shared, &decode_shared
};

const stl_type_descriptor_t WEAK_DESCRIPTOR =
{
  WEAK_KIND.key, WEAK_KIND.display_name,
  &matches_weak, &build_weak, &decode_weak
};

} // namespace

void register_stl_shared_ptr_descriptors(std::vector<const stl_type_descriptor_t *> *out)
{
  out->push_back(&SHARED_DESCRIPTOR);
  out->push_back(&WEAK_DESCRIPTOR);
}

} // namespace stl_utility

```

`stl-utility/src/stl_shared_ptr_release.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "stl_shared_ptr_release.h"

#include <typeinf.hpp>

#include <cstring>
#include <set>

namespace stl_utility
{

namespace
{

constexpr uint32 REP_OFFSET = 0x08; // shared_ptr/weak_ptr's _Rep member offset

const cexpr_t *strip_casts(const cexpr_t *e)
{
  while ( e != nullptr && e->op == cot_cast )
    e = e->x;
  return e;
}

bool is_num32(const cexpr_t *e, uint32 value)
{
  return e != nullptr && e->op == cot_num && uint32(e->numval()) == value;
}

// True if `e` is `obj._Rep` / `obj->_Rep` (a memref/memptr at REP_OFFSET).
// On success, *base is the shared_ptr/weak_ptr object expression to embed
// in the synthetic call.
bool match_rep_member(const cexpr_t *e, const cexpr_t **base)
{
  e = strip_casts(e);
  if ( e == nullptr || (e->op != cot_memref && e->op != cot_memptr) || e->m != REP_OFFSET )
    return false;
  *base = e->x;
  return true;
}

// Shared shape-check for both match_decrement_check() and
// match_decrement_check_at() below. Returns the inner
// `_InterlockedExchangeAdd(...)` call node if `e` is
// `_InterlockedExchangeAdd(ANY_PTR_EXPR, 0xFFFFFFFF) == 1`, else nullptr.
const cexpr_t *extract_decrement_call(const cexpr_t *e)
{
  e = strip_casts(e);
  if ( e == nullptr || e->op != cot_eq )
    return nullptr;

  const cexpr_t *a = strip_casts(e->x);
  const cexpr_t *b = strip_casts(e->y);
  const cexpr_t *call = nullptr;
  if ( is_num32(a, 1) )
    call = b;
  else if ( is_num32(b, 1) )
    call = a;
  if ( call == nullptr )
    return nullptr;

  call = strip_casts(call);
  if ( call == nullptr || call->op != cot_call || call->a == nullptr || call->a->size() != 2 )
    return nullptr;

  const cexpr_t *callee = strip_casts(call->x);
  if ( callee == nullptr || callee->op != cot_helper || callee->helper == nullptr )
    return nullptr;
  if ( std::strcmp(callee->helper, "_InterlockedExchangeAdd") != 0 )
    return nullptr;

  if ( !is_num32(strip_casts(&call->a->at(1)), 0xFFFFFFFFu) )
    return nullptr;

  return call;
}

// True if `e` is `_InterlockedExchangeAdd(ANY_PTR_EXPR, 0xFFFFFFFF) == 1`.
bool match_decrement_check(const cexpr_t *e)
{
  return extract_decrement_call(e) != nullptr;
}

bool same_expr(const cexpr_t *a, const cexpr_t *b)
{
  a = strip_casts(a);
  b = strip_casts(b);
  if ( a == nullptr || b == nullptr )
    return false;
  return a->equal_effect(*b);
}

// True if `e` is structurally `base + units` (or `units + base`), tolerating
// cast wrapping on either operand -- e.g. `base` being a `volatile signed
// __int32 *` and `units` being 2, matching `base + 2` in that pointer's own
// element units (== +8 bytes, i.e. `_Uses`'s offset).
bool match_ptr_plus_units(const cexpr_t *e, const cexpr_t *base, uint32 units)
{
  e = strip_casts(e);
  if ( e == nullptr || e->op != cot_add )
    return false;

  const cexpr_t *x = strip_casts(e->x);
  const cexpr_t *y = strip_casts(e->y);
  if ( is_num32(y, units) )
    return same_expr(x, base);
  if ( is_num32(x, units) )
    return same_expr(y, base);
  return false;
}

// Like match_decrement_check(), but additionally requires the call's first
// argument to be structurally `base + units` (tolerating casts). Used for
// the bare-pointer cot_land shape below, where there's no `._Rep` member
// access to anchor the match on, so we must verify the pointer arithmetic
// explicitly instead.
bool match_decrement_check_at(const cexpr_t *e, const cexpr_t *base, uint32 units)
{
  const cexpr_t *call = extract_decrement_call(e);
  if ( call == nullptr )
    return false;
  return match_ptr_plus_units(&call->a->at(0), base, units);
}

// If `branch` is a single cit_expr/cit_if directly, returns it; if it's a
// cit_block containing exactly one such top-level statement, returns that;
// otherwise nullptr.
cinsn_t *single_statement(cinsn_t *branch, ctype_t expected_op)
{
  if ( branch == nullptr )
    return nullptr;
  if ( branch->op == expected_op )
    return branch;
  if ( branch->op != cit_block || branch->cblock == nullptr )
    return nullptr;

  cinsn_t *found = nullptr;
  size_t count = 0;
  for ( cinsn_t &stmt : *branch->cblock )
  {
    ++count;
    if ( stmt.op == expected_op )
      found = &stmt;
  }
  return count == 1 ? found : nullptr;
}

struct release_match_t
{
  cinsn_t *matched_stmt = nullptr;
  const cexpr_t *sp_expr = nullptr;
};

bool try_match_release(cinsn_t *outer_if, release_match_t *out)
{
  if ( outer_if == nullptr || outer_if->op != cit_if || outer_if->cif == nullptr )
    return false;
  if ( outer_if->cif->ielse != nullptr )
    return false;

  const cexpr_t *sp_expr = nullptr;
  if ( !match_rep_member(&outer_if->cif->expr, &sp_expr) )
  {
    // Second valid shape: the owning object was never retyped to a
    // shared_ptr-bearing struct, so there's no "obj._Rep" member access --
    // the control-block pointer is already a bare raw pointer local (e.g.
    // "v30"), and the null-check + _Uses-decrement check are combined into
    // a single `cot_land` (`P && decrement_check(P + 2)`) instead of two
    // nested `if`s:
    //
    //   if ( v30 && _InterlockedExchangeAdd(v30 + 2, 0xFFFFFFFF) == 1 )
    //
    // Here `sp_expr` becomes `P` itself (the bare pointer), since there's no
    // `_Rep` member to embed -- the synthesized helper is `~shared_ptr(P)`.
    const cexpr_t *land = strip_casts(&outer_if->cif->expr);
    if ( land != nullptr && land->op == cot_land )
    {
      const cexpr_t *ptr_expr = strip_casts(land->x);
      if ( ptr_expr == nullptr )
        return false;
      if ( !match_decrement_check_at(land->y, ptr_expr, 2) )
        return false;

      sp_expr = ptr_expr;
    }
    else
    {
      // Third valid shape (confirmed via sub_140452240 in ScrapMechanic.exe):
      // same bare-pointer situation as the cot_land shape above (no "._Rep"
      // member to anchor on), but here the null-check and the _Uses-
      // decrement check are NOT combined into one `cot_land` condition --
      // they're two separate nested `if`s, same structure as the first
      // (typed) shape, just with a bare pointer instead of "obj._Rep":
      //
      //   if ( v8 )
      //   {
      //     if ( _InterlockedExchangeAdd(v8 + 2, 0xFFFFFFFF) == 1 )
      //     {
      //       (**(void (__fastcall ***)(volatile signed __int32 *))v8)(v8);
      //       if ( _InterlockedExchangeAdd(v8 + 3, 0xFFFFFFFF) == 1 )
      //         v8->VTABLE[0x1]();
      //     }
      //   }
      //
      // The outer condition here is just the bare pointer expression itself
      // (`v8`), stripped of casts -- there's nothing further to validate
      // about its shape; the existing strict uses_if/uses_body checks below
      // (identical to the first shape's) provide the safety margin, since
      // uses_if must independently pass match_decrement_check() and
      // uses_body must have exactly a destroy-call statement plus a
      // weaks_if that itself passes match_decrement_check().
      const cexpr_t *ptr_expr = strip_casts(&outer_if->cif->expr);
      if ( ptr_expr == nullptr )
        return false;

      sp_expr = ptr_expr;
    }
  }

  cinsn_t *uses_if = single_statement(outer_if->cif->ithen, cit_if);
  if ( uses_if == nullptr || uses_if->cif == nullptr || uses_if->cif->ielse != nullptr )
    return false;
  if ( !match_decrement_check(&uses_if->cif->expr) )
    return false;

  cinsn_t *uses_body = uses_if->cif->ithen;
  if ( uses_body == nullptr || uses_body->op != cit_block || uses_body->cblock == nullptr )
    return false;

  cinsn_t *destroy_call = nullptr;
  cinsn_t *weaks_if = nullptr;
  size_t stmt_count = 0;
  for ( cinsn_t &stmt : *uses_body->cblock )
  {
    ++stmt_count;
    if ( stmt.op == cit_expr )
      destroy_call = &stmt;
    else if ( stmt.op == cit_if )
      weaks_if = &stmt;
  }
  if ( stmt_count != 2 || destroy_call == nullptr || weaks_if == nullptr || weaks_if->cif == nullptr )
    return false;
  if ( weaks_if->cif->ielse != nullptr )
    return false;
  if ( !match_decrement_check(&weaks_if->cif->expr) )
    return false;

  cinsn_t *delete_call = single_statement(weaks_if->cif->ithen, cit_expr);
  if ( delete_call == nullptr )
    return false;

  out->matched_stmt = outer_if;
  out->sp_expr = sp_expr;
  return true;
}

bool synthesize_and_replace(const release_match_t &match)
{
  if ( match.matched_stmt == nullptr || match.sp_expr == nullptr )
    return false;

  tinfo_t void_type;
  if ( !void_type.create_simple_type(BTF_VOID) )
    return false;

  carglist_t *args = new carglist_t();
  args->resize(1);
  args->at(0).consume_cexpr(new cexpr_t(*match.sp_expr));

  // Render as a destructor-call-shaped helper, e.g. "~shared_ptr(sp_x)",
  // rather than a generic "release(sp_x)". Hex-Rays' call_helper()/
  // vcall_helper() (see hexrays.hpp) only ever print a helper expression as
  // "name(args...)" -- the name is an arbitrary string the decompiler prints
  // verbatim, not a real function, and there is no ctree construct available
  // to us that prints true member-destructor syntax ("obj.~Type()"); that
  // form only appears when Hex-Rays itself recognizes an actual destructor
  // call, which is not what we're synthesizing here. So "~shared_ptr(...)"
  // is the closest achievable approximation, and importantly the object
  // argument below is still a real cloned cexpr_t (not text baked into the
  // helper name), so it stays clickable/renameable in the pseudocode.
  //
  // Note: match_rep_member()/try_match_release() above only recognize the
  // "obj._Rep" member-offset pattern and never inspect the object's type,
  // so this matcher cannot distinguish shared_ptr from weak_ptr releases --
  // both render as "~shared_ptr(...)" here.
  cexpr_t *call = call_helper(void_type, args, "~shared_ptr");
  if ( call == nullptr )
    return false;

  cinsn_t *new_stmt = new cinsn_t();
  new_stmt->op = cit_expr;
  new_stmt->cexpr = call;
  new_stmt->ea = match.matched_stmt->ea;

  match.matched_stmt->replace_by(new_stmt);
  return true;
}

struct release_collapser_t : public ctree_visitor_t
{
  int collapsed_count = 0;

  release_collapser_t() : ctree_visitor_t(CV_FAST) {}

  int idaapi visit_insn(cinsn_t *insn) override
  {
    if ( insn->op != cit_if )
      return 0;

    release_match_t match;
    if ( !try_match_release(insn, &match) )
      return 0;
    if ( synthesize_and_replace(match) )
      ++collapsed_count;
    return 0;
  }
};

struct used_var_collector_t : public ctree_visitor_t
{
  std::set<int> used_indices;

  used_var_collector_t() : ctree_visitor_t(CV_FAST) {}

  int idaapi visit_expr(cexpr_t *e) override
  {
    if ( e->op == cot_var )
      used_indices.insert(e->v.idx);
    return 0;
  }
};

// See IDA-XXHash-Utility's xxhash_plugin.cpp for the rationale: replace_by
// deletes statements but leaves now-orphaned locals (the cached control
// block pointer, e.g. "v22") declared-but-unused, since their CVAR_USED
// flag isn't recalculated after our mutation. Hide anything no longer
// referenced, skipping arguments (never hide those -- it would alter the
// printed function signature, not just a declaration line).
void hide_orphaned_locals(cfunc_t *cfunc)
{
  used_var_collector_t collector;
  collector.apply_to(&cfunc->body, nullptr);

  lvars_t *lvars = cfunc->get_lvars();
  if ( lvars == nullptr )
    return;

  for ( size_t i = 0; i < lvars->size(); ++i )
  {
    lvar_t &lvar = lvars->at(i);
    if ( lvar.is_arg_var() || !lvar.used() )
      continue;
    if ( collector.used_indices.find(int(i)) == collector.used_indices.end() )
      lvar.clear_used();
  }
}

} // namespace

int collapse_shared_ptr_releases(cfunc_t *cfunc)
{
  release_collapser_t collapser;
  collapser.apply_to(&cfunc->body, nullptr);
  if ( collapser.collapsed_count > 0 )
    hide_orphaned_locals(cfunc);
  return collapser.collapsed_count;
}

} // namespace stl_utility

```

`stl-utility/src/stl_shared_ptr_release.h`:

```h
#pragma once

#include <hexrays.hpp>

namespace stl_utility
{

// Recognizes the inlined MSVC std::shared_ptr/weak_ptr release sequence:
//   if ( sp._Rep )
//   {
//     if ( _InterlockedExchangeAdd(&sp._Rep->_Uses, -1) == 1 )
//     {
//       sp._Rep->vtbl[0](sp._Rep);  // virtual destroy
//       if ( _InterlockedExchangeAdd(&sp._Rep->_Weaks, -1) == 1 )
//         sp._Rep->vtbl[1]();      // virtual delete-this
//     }
//   }
// This is pure ABI boilerplate emitted at every shared_ptr scope exit --
// not useful to a reverse engineer -- so it's collapsed into a single
// `release(sp);`-shaped synthetic call, mirroring IDA-XXHash-Utility's
// approach to collapsing inlined algorithm boilerplate. Runs at
// hxe_maturity/CMAT_FINAL, mutating the ctree via cinsn_t::replace_by
// before pseudocode text is generated.
//
// Returns the number of collapsed occurrences within `cfunc`'s body.
int collapse_shared_ptr_releases(cfunc_t *cfunc);

} // namespace stl_utility

```

`stl-utility/src/stl_string.cpp`:

```cpp
// MSVC std::basic_string<char>/<wchar_t> descriptor: the SSO-union pain
// point this plugin exists to solve. Layout (x64, VS2019/2022 ABI), per
// microsoft/STL's xstring:
//
//   struct basic_string {                 // sizeof == 0x20
//     union _Bxty {                       // offset 0x00, size 16
//       value_type _Buf[_BUF_SIZE];        // inline SSO storage
//       value_type *_Ptr;                  // heap pointer
//     } _Bx;
//     size_t _Mysize;                      // offset 0x10 -- length in chars
//     size_t _Myres;                       // offset 0x18 -- capacity in chars
//   };
//
// _BUF_SIZE is 16 for char, 8 for wchar_t. The union's active arm is chosen
// by capacity, not length: _Myres < _BUF_SIZE means the inline buffer is
// live; otherwise _Bx._Ptr is the heap pointer.

#include "stl_registry.h"
#include "stl_memory.h"
#include "stl_util.h"

#include <typeinf.hpp>

#include <cstdint>
#include <cstring>
#include <string>

namespace stl_utility
{

namespace
{

constexpr size_t STRUCT_SIZE = 0x20;
constexpr size_t MYSIZE_OFFSET = 0x10;
constexpr size_t MYRES_OFFSET = 0x18;
constexpr size_t MAX_PREVIEW_CHARS = 200;

struct string_kind_t
{
  bool is_wide;
  size_t element_size;   // 1 for char, 2 for wchar_t
  size_t buf_size;       // SSO threshold in elements: 16 for char, 8 for wchar_t
  const char *key;
  const char *display_name;
  const char *synthetic_name;
  const char *real_names[3]; // candidate exact STL names to look up first, nullptr-terminated
};

const string_kind_t CHAR_KIND =
{
  false, 1, 16,
  "std_string_char",
  "std::string (char)",
  "__stl_string_char_sso",
  { "std::basic_string<char,std::char_traits<char>,std::allocator<char>>", "std::string", nullptr }
};

const string_kind_t WCHAR_KIND =
{
  true, 2, 8,
  "std_string_wchar",
  "std::wstring (wchar_t)",
  "__stl_string_wchar_sso",
  { "std::basic_string<wchar_t,std::char_traits<wchar_t>,std::allocator<wchar_t>>", "std::wstring", nullptr }
};

bool build_element_type(const string_kind_t &kind, tinfo_t *out)
{
  return out->create_simple_type(kind.is_wide ? BTF_UINT16 : BTF_CHAR);
}

// Builds the plugin-owned synthetic struct/union layout described above and
// registers it into Local Types under `kind.synthetic_name`.
bool build_synthetic_type(const string_kind_t &kind, tinfo_t *out_type, qstring *out_name)
{
  tinfo_t element;
  if ( !build_element_type(kind, &element) )
    return false;

  tinfo_t buf_array;
  if ( !buf_array.create_array(element, uint32(kind.buf_size)) )
    return false;

  tinfo_t ptr_type;
  if ( !ptr_type.create_ptr(element) )
    return false;

  udt_type_data_t union_data;
  union_data.is_union = true;
  union_data.add_member("_Buf", buf_array, 0);
  union_data.add_member("_Ptr", ptr_type, 0);
  tinfo_t union_type;
  if ( !union_type.create_udt(union_data) )
    return false;

  tinfo_t size_type;
  if ( !size_type.create_simple_type(BTF_UINT64) )
    return false;

  udt_type_data_t struct_data;
  struct_data.is_union = false;
  struct_data.add_member("_Bx", union_type, 0);
  struct_data.add_member("_Mysize", size_type, MYSIZE_OFFSET * 8);
  struct_data.add_member("_Myres", size_type, MYRES_OFFSET * 8);

  tinfo_t struct_type;
  if ( !struct_type.create_udt(struct_data) )
    return false;

  if ( struct_type.set_named_type(nullptr, kind.synthetic_name, NTF_REPLACE) != TERR_OK )
    return false;

  tinfo_t saved;
  if ( saved.get_named_type(nullptr, kind.synthetic_name, BTF_STRUCT) && saved.get_size() == STRUCT_SIZE )
  {
    *out_type = saved;
    *out_name = kind.synthetic_name;
    return true;
  }

  *out_type = struct_type;
  *out_name = kind.synthetic_name;
  return true;
}

bool build_or_get_type_impl(const string_kind_t &kind, tinfo_t *out_type, qstring *out_name)
{
  for ( int i = 0; kind.real_names[i] != nullptr; ++i )
  {
    tinfo_t candidate;
    if ( candidate.get_named_type(nullptr, kind.real_names[i], BTF_STRUCT)
      && candidate.get_size() == STRUCT_SIZE )
    {
      *out_type = candidate;
      *out_name = kind.real_names[i];
      debug_log("stl_string: reusing existing Local Type '%s'", kind.real_names[i]);
      return true;
    }
  }

  debug_log("stl_string: building synthetic type '%s'", kind.synthetic_name);
  return build_synthetic_type(kind, out_type, out_name);
}

match_confidence_t matches_impl(
      const string_kind_t &kind,
      const tinfo_t *existing_type,
      const qstring *demangled_name_hint)
{
  if ( demangled_name_hint != nullptr && demangled_name_hint->find("basic_string") != qstring::npos )
  {
    const bool hint_is_wide =
      demangled_name_hint->find("wchar_t") != qstring::npos
      || demangled_name_hint->find("wstring") != qstring::npos;
    return hint_is_wide == kind.is_wide ? MATCH_HIGH : MATCH_NONE;
  }

  // Recognize our own previously-applied synthetic type by name, so the
  // content preview keeps rendering on every subsequent decompile without
  // requiring the user to re-run the retype action.
  if ( demangled_name_hint != nullptr && *demangled_name_hint == kind.synthetic_name )
    return MATCH_HIGH;

  // Structural fallback: an opaque/undecoded 0x20-byte buffer is exactly
  // the case this plugin targets (Hex-Rays showing raw bytes because it
  // can't resolve the SSO union). We can't tell char from wchar_t purely
  // from size, so both kinds report ambiguous here and the retype action's
  // chooser lets the user pick.
  if ( existing_type != nullptr && !existing_type->empty() && existing_type->get_size() == STRUCT_SIZE )
    return MATCH_AMBIGUOUS;

  return MATCH_NONE;
}

std::string format_preview(const uint8_t *bytes, size_t byte_count, bool is_wide, bool truncated)
{
  std::string out = "\"";
  const size_t char_count = is_wide ? byte_count / 2 : byte_count;
  for ( size_t i = 0; i < char_count; ++i )
  {
    uint32_t code;
    if ( is_wide )
      code = uint32_t(bytes[i * 2]) | (uint32_t(bytes[i * 2 + 1]) << 8);
    else
      code = bytes[i];

    if ( code == '"' || code == '\\' )
    {
      out.push_back('\\');
      out.push_back(char(code));
    }
    else if ( code == '\n' )
    {
      out += "\\n";
    }
    else if ( code == '\r' )
    {
      out += "\\r";
    }
    else if ( code == '\t' )
    {
      out += "\\t";
    }
    else if ( code >= 0x20 && code < 0x7F )
    {
      out.push_back(char(code));
    }
    else
    {
      char buf[8];
      qsnprintf(buf, sizeof(buf), "\\x%02X", code & 0xFF);
      out += buf;
    }
  }
  out.push_back('"');
  if ( truncated )
    out += "...";
  return out;
}

stl_decode_result_t decode_preview_impl(const string_kind_t &kind, ea_t instance_ea, const tinfo_t &)
{
  stl_decode_result_t result;

  if ( instance_ea == BADADDR )
  {
    result.ok = true;
    result.preview = "(no fixed address)";
    return result;
  }

  const stl_read_result_t header = stl_read_memory(instance_ea, STRUCT_SIZE);
  if ( !header.ok )
  {
    result.ok = true;
    result.preview = looks_like_stack_address(instance_ea)
      ? qstring("(stack-local, attach debugger to preview)")
      : qstring("(unable to read memory)");
    return result;
  }

  uint64_t mysize = 0;
  uint64_t myres = 0;
  std::memcpy(&mysize, header.data.begin() + MYSIZE_OFFSET, sizeof(mysize));
  std::memcpy(&myres, header.data.begin() + MYRES_OFFSET, sizeof(myres));

  result.from_live_memory = header.from_debugger;

  const size_t element_size = kind.element_size;
  const size_t char_count = size_t(mysize < MAX_PREVIEW_CHARS ? mysize : MAX_PREVIEW_CHARS);
  const bool truncated = mysize > MAX_PREVIEW_CHARS;

  if ( myres < kind.buf_size )
  {
    // SSO arm: the string bytes live inline in _Bx._Buf.
    const size_t byte_count = char_count * element_size;
    result.ok = true;
    result.preview.sprnt(
      "%s (size=%" FMT_64 "u cap=%" FMT_64 "u, inline)",
      format_preview(header.data.begin(), byte_count, kind.is_wide, truncated).c_str(),
      uint64(mysize),
      uint64(myres));
    return result;
  }

  // Heap arm: _Bx._Ptr holds the pointer to the allocation.
  ea_t heap_ptr = BADADDR;
  std::memcpy(&heap_ptr, header.data.begin(), sizeof(ea_t));

  if ( char_count == 0 )
  {
    result.ok = true;
    result.preview.sprnt(
      "\"\" (size=%" FMT_64 "u cap=%" FMT_64 "u, heap)",
      uint64(mysize),
      uint64(myres));
    return result;
  }

  const stl_read_result_t content =
    heap_ptr == BADADDR ? stl_read_result_t{} : stl_read_memory(heap_ptr, char_count * element_size);
  if ( !content.ok )
  {
    result.ok = true;
    result.preview.sprnt(
      "ptr=%a size=%" FMT_64 "u cap=%" FMT_64 "u (heap, attach debugger to preview)",
      heap_ptr,
      uint64(mysize),
      uint64(myres));
    return result;
  }

  result.from_live_memory = result.from_live_memory || content.from_debugger;
  result.ok = true;
  result.preview.sprnt(
    "%s (size=%" FMT_64 "u cap=%" FMT_64 "u, heap)",
    format_preview(content.data.begin(), content.data.size(), kind.is_wide, truncated).c_str(),
    uint64(mysize),
    uint64(myres));
  return result;
}

match_confidence_t matches_char(const tinfo_t *t, const qstring *hint) { return matches_impl(CHAR_KIND, t, hint); }
match_confidence_t matches_wchar(const tinfo_t *t, const qstring *hint) { return matches_impl(WCHAR_KIND, t, hint); }
bool build_char(til_t *, const tinfo_t *, const qstring *, tinfo_t *out_type, qstring *out_name) { return build_or_get_type_impl(CHAR_KIND, out_type, out_name); }
bool build_wchar(til_t *, const tinfo_t *, const qstring *, tinfo_t *out_type, qstring *out_name) { return build_or_get_type_impl(WCHAR_KIND, out_type, out_name); }
stl_decode_result_t decode_char(ea_t ea, const tinfo_t &t) { return decode_preview_impl(CHAR_KIND, ea, t); }
stl_decode_result_t decode_wchar(ea_t ea, const tinfo_t &t) { return decode_preview_impl(WCHAR_KIND, ea, t); }

const stl_type_descriptor_t CHAR_DESCRIPTOR =
{
  CHAR_KIND.key, CHAR_KIND.display_name,
  &matches_char, &build_char, &decode_char
};

const stl_type_descriptor_t WCHAR_DESCRIPTOR =
{
  WCHAR_KIND.key, WCHAR_KIND.display_name,
  &matches_wchar, &build_wchar, &decode_wchar
};

} // namespace

void register_stl_string_descriptors(std::vector<const stl_type_descriptor_t *> *out)
{
  out->push_back(&CHAR_DESCRIPTOR);
  out->push_back(&WCHAR_DESCRIPTOR);
}

} // namespace stl_utility

```

`stl-utility/src/stl_string_release.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "stl_string_release.h"

#include <typeinf.hpp>

#include <cstring>
#include <set>
#include <vector>

namespace stl_utility
{

namespace
{

// basic_string layout, per src/stl_string.cpp: _Bx at 0x00, _Mysize at 0x10,
// _Myres at 0x18, struct size 0x20.
constexpr uint32 MYRES_OFFSET = 0x18;

const cexpr_t *strip_casts(const cexpr_t *e)
{
  while ( e != nullptr && e->op == cot_cast )
    e = e->x;
  return e;
}

bool is_num32(const cexpr_t *e, uint32 value)
{
  return e != nullptr && e->op == cot_num && uint32(e->numval()) == value;
}

// True if `e` is `obj.<member at `offset`>` (a memref/memptr at `offset`).
// On success, *base is the object expression (`obj`).
bool match_member(const cexpr_t *e, uint32 offset, const cexpr_t **base)
{
  e = strip_casts(e);
  if ( e == nullptr || (e->op != cot_memref && e->op != cot_memptr) || e->m != offset )
    return false;
  *base = e->x;
  return true;
}

// True if `e` is `obj._Myres > 0xF` (signed or unsigned greater-than against
// the literal 0xF). This is MSVC's "is this string NOT using the inline SSO
// buffer" check -- the strict, distinctive backstop for this whole matcher.
// On success, *base is the string object expression (`obj`).
bool match_myres_gt_sso_threshold(const cexpr_t *e, const cexpr_t **base)
{
  e = strip_casts(e);
  if ( e == nullptr || (e->op != cot_sgt && e->op != cot_ugt) )
    return false;

  const cexpr_t *lhs = strip_casts(e->x);
  if ( !match_member(lhs, MYRES_OFFSET, base) )
    return false;

  return is_num32(strip_casts(e->y), 0xF);
}

// True if `parent_type` is a UDT (struct/union) with a member named
// `member_name` whose slot matches `access_m` under the access semantics
// Hex-Rays uses for that UDT kind. Per hexrays.hpp's cexpr_t::m doc
// comment: "member offset (used for cot_memptr, cot_memref) ... for
// unions, the member number" -- i.e. `m` is a BYTE OFFSET when the
// immediate parent is a plain struct, but an ORDINAL MEMBER INDEX when the
// immediate parent is a union. Resolving by name (rather than assuming a
// fixed offset/index) keeps this correct regardless of which convention
// applies at this layer, and regardless of exact member ordering/offsets
// in whatever build produced the type.
bool udt_member_matches(const tinfo_t &parent_type, uint32 access_m, const char *member_name)
{
  if ( !parent_type.is_udt() )
    return false;

  udt_type_data_t udt;
  if ( !parent_type.get_udt_details(&udt) )
    return false;

  if ( udt.is_union )
  {
    // Union: `m` is the member's ordinal index into the declaration order.
    return access_m < udt.size() && udt[access_m].name == member_name;
  }

  // Plain struct: `m` is a byte offset. Find the named member and compare
  // its byte offset (udm_t::offset is in BITS) against `access_m`.
  for ( size_t i = 0; i < udt.size(); ++i )
  {
    if ( udt[i].name == member_name )
      return access_m == uint32(udt[i].offset / 8);
  }
  return false;
}

// True if `e` is `parent.<member_name>` (a memref/memptr node whose
// immediate parent's UDT member list resolves `member_name` at slot
// `e->m`, per udt_member_matches()'s struct-offset-vs-union-index
// handling). On success, *base is the parent object expression.
bool match_named_member(const cexpr_t *e, const char *member_name, const cexpr_t **base)
{
  e = strip_casts(e);
  if ( e == nullptr || (e->op != cot_memref && e->op != cot_memptr) )
    return false;

  const cexpr_t *parent = strip_casts(e->x);
  if ( parent == nullptr )
    return false;

  tinfo_t parent_type = parent->type;
  if ( e->op == cot_memptr && parent_type.is_ptr() )
    parent_type = parent_type.get_pointed_object();

  if ( !udt_member_matches(parent_type, e->m, member_name) )
    return false;

  *base = parent;
  return true;
}

// True if `e` resolves to `obj._Bx._Ptr` after stripping casts: a two-level
// member-access chain where the first layer (`_Bx`, a plain-struct member
// of basic_string) and the second layer (`_Ptr`, a member of the anonymous
// `_Bxty` union) are each matched by NAME via match_named_member() --
// critically, NOT by assuming both layers use the same "cumulative byte
// offset" convention, since the union layer's `e->m` is an ordinal member
// index, not a byte offset (see match_named_member()/udt_member_matches()).
// Also tolerates Hex-Rays flattening the anonymous union access away
// entirely, in which case only one memref/memptr layer is present.
// On success, *base is the string object expression (`obj`).
bool match_bx_ptr_chain(const cexpr_t *e, const cexpr_t **base)
{
  const cexpr_t *ptr_base = nullptr;
  if ( match_named_member(e, "_Ptr", &ptr_base) )
  {
    const cexpr_t *bx_base = nullptr;
    if ( match_named_member(ptr_base, "_Bx", &bx_base) )
    {
      *base = bx_base;
      return true;
    }
    // Flattened case: Hex-Rays collapsed the `_Bx` union access away, so
    // `ptr_base` is already the string object itself.
    *base = ptr_base;
    return true;
  }

  // Fully-flattened fallback: a single memref/memptr straight at `_Bx`
  // (e.g. if Hex-Rays instead collapses the `_Ptr` union arm access away).
  const cexpr_t *bx_base = nullptr;
  if ( match_named_member(e, "_Bx", &bx_base) )
  {
    *base = bx_base;
    return true;
  }

  return false;
}

bool same_object(const cexpr_t *a, const cexpr_t *b)
{
  a = strip_casts(a);
  b = strip_casts(b);
  if ( a == nullptr || b == nullptr )
    return false;
  return a->equal_effect(*b);
}

// True if `e` resolves (tolerating Hex-Rays flattening the anonymous union
// access away, same fallback structure as match_bx_ptr_chain()) to
// `obj._Bx._Buf`. On success, *base is the string object expression (`obj`).
bool match_bx_buf_chain(const cexpr_t *e, const cexpr_t **base)
{
  const cexpr_t *buf_base = nullptr;
  if ( match_named_member(e, "_Buf", &buf_base) )
  {
    const cexpr_t *bx_base = nullptr;
    if ( match_named_member(buf_base, "_Bx", &bx_base) )
    {
      *base = bx_base;
      return true;
    }
    // Flattened case: Hex-Rays collapsed the `_Bx` union access away.
    *base = buf_base;
    return true;
  }

  // Fully-flattened fallback: a single memref/memptr straight at `_Bx`.
  const cexpr_t *bx_base = nullptr;
  if ( match_named_member(e, "_Bx", &bx_base) )
  {
    *base = bx_base;
    return true;
  }

  return false;
}

// True if `sib` is `obj.<member_name> = value` (a cit_expr wrapping a
// cot_asg) for the SAME object as `obj_expr` (per same_object()). Used to
// match the `_Mysize = 0` / `_Myres = 0xF` tail reset writes.
bool match_named_member_reset_write(
        const cinsn_t *sib,
        const cexpr_t *obj_expr,
        const char *member_name,
        uint32 value)
{
  if ( sib == nullptr || sib->op != cit_expr || sib->cexpr == nullptr )
    return false;

  const cexpr_t *asg = strip_casts(sib->cexpr);
  if ( asg == nullptr || asg->op != cot_asg )
    return false;

  const cexpr_t *base = nullptr;
  if ( !match_named_member(asg->x, member_name, &base) )
    return false;
  if ( !same_object(base, obj_expr) )
    return false;

  return is_num32(strip_casts(asg->y), value);
}

// True if `sib` is `obj._Bx._Buf[0] = 0` (the SSO buffer's first-byte
// null-terminator reset), for the SAME object as `obj_expr`.
bool match_buf_zero_write(const cinsn_t *sib, const cexpr_t *obj_expr)
{
  if ( sib == nullptr || sib->op != cit_expr || sib->cexpr == nullptr )
    return false;

  const cexpr_t *asg = strip_casts(sib->cexpr);
  if ( asg == nullptr || asg->op != cot_asg )
    return false;

  const cexpr_t *lhs = strip_casts(asg->x);
  if ( lhs == nullptr || lhs->op != cot_idx )
    return false;
  if ( !is_num32(strip_casts(lhs->y), 0) )
    return false;

  const cexpr_t *base = nullptr;
  if ( !match_bx_buf_chain(lhs->x, &base) )
    return false;
  if ( !same_object(base, obj_expr) )
    return false;

  return is_num32(strip_casts(asg->y), 0);
}

struct string_match_t
{
  cinsn_t *matched_stmt = nullptr;
  const cexpr_t *obj_expr = nullptr;
};

bool try_match_string_release(cinsn_t *outer_if, string_match_t *out)
{
  if ( outer_if == nullptr || outer_if->op != cit_if || outer_if->cif == nullptr )
    return false;
  if ( outer_if->cif->ielse != nullptr )
    return false;

  const cexpr_t *obj_expr = nullptr;
  if ( !match_myres_gt_sso_threshold(&outer_if->cif->expr, &obj_expr) )
    return false;

  cinsn_t *body = outer_if->cif->ithen;
  if ( body == nullptr || body->op != cit_block || body->cblock == nullptr )
    return false;

  std::vector<cinsn_t *> stmts;
  for ( cinsn_t &stmt : *body->cblock )
    stmts.push_back(&stmt);

  // Expected shape: [ptr = obj._Bx._Ptr;] (optional big-alloc-header
  // validation `if`;) free_call(ptr); -- i.e. 2 or 3 top-level statements.
  if ( stmts.size() != 2 && stmts.size() != 3 )
    return false;

  cinsn_t *assign_stmt = stmts.front();
  if ( assign_stmt->op != cit_expr || assign_stmt->cexpr == nullptr )
    return false;
  const cexpr_t *assign = assign_stmt->cexpr;
  if ( assign->op != cot_asg )
    return false;

  const cexpr_t *rhs_base = nullptr;
  if ( !match_bx_ptr_chain(assign->y, &rhs_base) )
    return false;
  if ( !same_object(obj_expr, rhs_base) )
    return false;

  cinsn_t *free_stmt = stmts.back();
  if ( free_stmt->op != cit_expr || free_stmt->cexpr == nullptr )
    return false;
  if ( strip_casts(free_stmt->cexpr)->op != cot_call )
    return false;

  // Middle statement (when present) is MSVC's big-allocation-header
  // validation `if`. Its exact inner shape (the 0x1000/0x1F constants, the
  // noreturn call) is deliberately NOT validated -- it's optional
  // boilerplate that only fires for very large allocations / checked
  // builds. We just require it to be an `if` so an unrelated statement
  // here doesn't get silently swallowed into the match.
  if ( stmts.size() == 3 && stmts[1]->op != cit_if )
    return false;

  out->matched_stmt = outer_if;
  out->obj_expr = obj_expr;
  return true;
}

// Best-effort std::string vs std::wstring distinction: if the object's
// resolved type name (set up by src/stl_string.cpp's retyping, e.g. the
// synthetic "__stl_string_wchar_sso" struct, or a real demangled
// std::wstring/basic_string<wchar_t,...> name) mentions "wchar"/"wstring",
// render as "~wstring(...)"; otherwise default to "~string(...)". If the
// object hasn't been retyped yet (still an opaque byte buffer / unresolved
// type), this can't distinguish char from wchar_t and falls back to
// "~string(...)".
bool looks_like_wide_string(const cexpr_t *obj_expr)
{
  if ( obj_expr == nullptr )
    return false;

  qstring type_name;
  if ( !obj_expr->type.print(&type_name) )
    return false;

  return type_name.find("wchar") != qstring::npos
      || type_name.find("wstring") != qstring::npos;
}

bool synthesize_and_replace(const string_match_t &match)
{
  if ( match.matched_stmt == nullptr || match.obj_expr == nullptr )
    return false;

  tinfo_t void_type;
  if ( !void_type.create_simple_type(BTF_VOID) )
    return false;

  carglist_t *args = new carglist_t();
  args->resize(1);
  args->at(0).consume_cexpr(new cexpr_t(*match.obj_expr));

  // Same rendering approach as stl_shared_ptr_release.cpp's
  // synthesize_and_replace(): a helper expression printed as "name(args...)"
  // is the closest achievable approximation of true "obj.~Type()" syntax
  // (Hex-Rays has no ctree construct for synthesizing the latter), and the
  // object argument is a real cloned cexpr_t -- clickable/renameable in the
  // pseudocode, not text baked into the helper name.
  const char *helper_name = looks_like_wide_string(match.obj_expr) ? "~wstring" : "~string";
  cexpr_t *call = call_helper(void_type, args, helper_name);
  if ( call == nullptr )
    return false;

  cinsn_t *new_stmt = new cinsn_t();
  new_stmt->op = cit_expr;
  new_stmt->cexpr = call;
  new_stmt->ea = match.matched_stmt->ea;

  match.matched_stmt->replace_by(new_stmt);
  return true;
}

// Scans whole cblock_t's (rather than single cit_if nodes in isolation) so
// it can see and remove the up-to-3 sibling reset-writes
// ("boneName._Mysize = 0; boneName._Myres = 15; boneName._Bx._Buf[0] = 0;")
// that MSVC emits immediately after the matched `if` -- these are true
// siblings in the surrounding block, not descendants of the `if`, so a
// single-node visit_insn(cit_if) override structurally cannot reach them.
struct string_release_collapser_t : public ctree_visitor_t
{
  int collapsed_count = 0;

  string_release_collapser_t() : ctree_visitor_t(CV_FAST) {}

  int idaapi visit_insn(cinsn_t *insn) override
  {
    if ( insn->op != cit_block || insn->cblock == nullptr )
      return 0;

    cblock_t &block = *insn->cblock;
    for ( cblock_t::iterator it = block.begin(); it != block.end(); ++it )
    {
      cinsn_t *stmt = &*it;
      if ( stmt->op != cit_if )
        continue;

      string_match_t match;
      if ( !try_match_string_release(stmt, &match) )
        continue;

      // Look at (up to) the next 3 sibling statements in the SAME block
      // for the reset-tail writes. Tolerate any subset and any order --
      // each candidate is tested against whichever of the three known
      // writes hasn't been consumed yet -- but never erase a sibling that
      // doesn't positively match one of them.
      bool consumed_size = false;
      bool consumed_res = false;
      bool consumed_buf = false;

      cblock_t::iterator scan = it;
      ++scan;
      for ( int i = 0; i < 3 && scan != block.end(); ++i )
      {
        cinsn_t *sib = &*scan;
        bool matched_reset = false;

        if ( !consumed_size && match_named_member_reset_write(sib, match.obj_expr, "_Mysize", 0) )
        {
          consumed_size = true;
          matched_reset = true;
        }
        else if ( !consumed_res && match_named_member_reset_write(sib, match.obj_expr, "_Myres", 0xF) )
        {
          consumed_res = true;
          matched_reset = true;
        }
        else if ( !consumed_buf && match_buf_zero_write(sib, match.obj_expr) )
        {
          consumed_buf = true;
          matched_reset = true;
        }

        if ( matched_reset )
        {
          cblock_t::iterator to_erase = scan;
          ++scan;
          block.erase(to_erase);
        }
        else
        {
          ++scan;
        }
      }

      if ( synthesize_and_replace(match) )
        ++collapsed_count;
    }
    return 0;
  }
};

struct used_var_collector_t : public ctree_visitor_t
{
  std::set<int> used_indices;

  used_var_collector_t() : ctree_visitor_t(CV_FAST) {}

  int idaapi visit_expr(cexpr_t *e) override
  {
    if ( e->op == cot_var )
      used_indices.insert(e->v.idx);
    return 0;
  }
};

// See stl_shared_ptr_release.cpp's hide_orphaned_locals() for the
// rationale: replace_by deletes statements but leaves now-orphaned locals
// (the cached heap pointer, e.g. "Ptr") declared-but-unused, since their
// CVAR_USED flag isn't recalculated after our mutation. Hide anything no
// longer referenced, skipping arguments (never hide those -- it would
// alter the printed function signature, not just a declaration line).
void hide_orphaned_locals(cfunc_t *cfunc)
{
  used_var_collector_t collector;
  collector.apply_to(&cfunc->body, nullptr);

  lvars_t *lvars = cfunc->get_lvars();
  if ( lvars == nullptr )
    return;

  for ( size_t i = 0; i < lvars->size(); ++i )
  {
    lvar_t &lvar = lvars->at(i);
    if ( lvar.is_arg_var() || !lvar.used() )
      continue;
    if ( collector.used_indices.find(int(i)) == collector.used_indices.end() )
      lvar.clear_used();
  }
}

} // namespace

int collapse_string_releases(cfunc_t *cfunc)
{
  string_release_collapser_t collapser;
  collapser.apply_to(&cfunc->body, nullptr);
  if ( collapser.collapsed_count > 0 )
    hide_orphaned_locals(cfunc);
  return collapser.collapsed_count;
}

} // namespace stl_utility

```

`stl-utility/src/stl_string_release.h`:

```h
#pragma once

#include <hexrays.hpp>

namespace stl_utility
{

// Recognizes the inlined MSVC std::basic_string (std::string/std::wstring)
// scope-exit release sequence -- the conditional heap-free that only runs
// when the string wasn't using small-string-optimization (SSO). See
// src/stl_string.cpp for the established basic_string layout (_Bx at 0x00,
// _Mysize at 0x10, _Myres at 0x18, struct size 0x20):
//
//   if ( obj._Myres > 0xF )                          // NOT using inline SSO buffer
//   {
//     ptr = obj._Bx._Ptr;
//     if ( obj._Myres + 1 >= 0x1000 )                 // OPTIONAL: big-allocation
//     {                                                // header validation, only
//       ptr = *((char **)obj._Bx._Ptr - 1);            // emitted for very large
//       if ( (unsigned __int64)(obj._Bx._Ptr - ptr - 8) > 0x1F )
//         invalid_parameter_noinfo_noreturn();          // allocations / checked builds
//     }
//     free_or_delete_call(ptr);
//   }
//
// This is pure ABI boilerplate emitted at every std::string/std::wstring
// scope exit -- not useful to a reverse engineer -- so it's collapsed into
// a single `~string(obj);`-shaped synthetic call, mirroring
// stl_shared_ptr_release.cpp's approach for shared_ptr/weak_ptr release.
// Runs at hxe_maturity/CMAT_FINAL, mutating the ctree via
// cinsn_t::replace_by before pseudocode text is generated.
//
// The inner big-allocation-header-validation `if` and the exact free/abort
// callee names are NOT required by the matcher (they vary across build
// configurations and may be absent entirely); the strict, distinctive
// backstop is the `_Myres > 0xF` / `_Bx._Ptr` member-access shape, mirroring
// how the shared_ptr matcher leans on its own `_Rep`/`_InterlockedExchangeAdd`
// backstop rather than validating callee names.
//
// Returns the number of collapsed occurrences within `cfunc`'s body.
int collapse_string_releases(cfunc_t *cfunc);

} // namespace stl_utility

```

`stl-utility/src/stl_tree.cpp`:

```cpp
// MSVC std::map/std::set/std::multimap/std::multiset descriptor -- v1
// STUB. These are red-black trees (_Tree<_Traits>) with per-node
// {_Left,_Parent,_Right,_Color,_Isnil,_Myval} layout that varies with the
// templated value type at a variable offset; full node/iterator traversal
// is deferred to v2. For now this only exposes the tree's header:
//
//   struct _Tree_val_header {   // sizeof == 0x10 (approximate/generic)
//     _Tree_node *_Myhead;      // offset 0x00 -- sentinel/root anchor
//     size_t _Mysize;           // offset 0x08 -- element count
//   };
//
// giving a one-click retype that at least reports element count and the
// root/sentinel address, without attempting to walk the tree.

#include "stl_registry.h"
#include "stl_memory.h"
#include "stl_util.h"

#include <typeinf.hpp>

#include <cstring>

namespace stl_utility
{

namespace
{

constexpr size_t STRUCT_SIZE = 0x10;
constexpr size_t MYHEAD_OFFSET = 0x00;
constexpr size_t MYSIZE_OFFSET = 0x08;
constexpr const char *SYNTHETIC_NAME = "__stl_tree_header_generic";

bool build_or_get_type_impl(til_t *, const tinfo_t *, const qstring *, tinfo_t *out_type, qstring *out_name)
{
  tinfo_t existing;
  if ( existing.get_named_type(nullptr, SYNTHETIC_NAME, BTF_STRUCT) && existing.get_size() == STRUCT_SIZE )
  {
    *out_type = existing;
    *out_name = SYNTHETIC_NAME;
    return true;
  }

  tinfo_t void_type, void_ptr, size_type;
  if ( !void_type.create_simple_type(BTF_VOID) || !void_ptr.create_ptr(void_type)
    || !size_type.create_simple_type(BTF_UINT64) )
  {
    return false;
  }

  udt_type_data_t p;
  p.is_union = false;
  p.add_member("_Myhead", void_ptr, MYHEAD_OFFSET * 8);
  p.add_member("_Mysize", size_type, MYSIZE_OFFSET * 8);

  tinfo_t built;
  if ( !built.create_udt(p) )
    return false;

  debug_log("stl_tree: building synthetic header type '%s'", SYNTHETIC_NAME);
  if ( built.set_named_type(nullptr, SYNTHETIC_NAME, NTF_REPLACE) != TERR_OK )
    return false;

  tinfo_t saved;
  if ( saved.get_named_type(nullptr, SYNTHETIC_NAME, BTF_STRUCT) && saved.get_size() == STRUCT_SIZE )
    *out_type = saved;
  else
    *out_type = built;
  *out_name = SYNTHETIC_NAME;
  return true;
}

match_confidence_t matches_impl(const tinfo_t *existing_type, const qstring *demangled_name_hint)
{
  if ( demangled_name_hint != nullptr )
  {
    static const char *const markers[] = { "map<", "set<", "multimap<", "multiset<" };
    for ( const char *marker : markers )
    {
      if ( demangled_name_hint->find(marker) != qstring::npos )
        return MATCH_HIGH;
    }

    if ( *demangled_name_hint == SYNTHETIC_NAME )
      return MATCH_HIGH;
  }

  if ( existing_type != nullptr && !existing_type->empty() && existing_type->get_size() == STRUCT_SIZE )
    return MATCH_AMBIGUOUS;

  return MATCH_NONE;
}

stl_decode_result_t decode_preview_impl(ea_t instance_ea, const tinfo_t &)
{
  stl_decode_result_t result;

  if ( instance_ea == BADADDR )
  {
    result.ok = true;
    result.preview = "(no fixed address)";
    return result;
  }

  const stl_read_result_t header = stl_read_memory(instance_ea, STRUCT_SIZE);
  if ( !header.ok )
  {
    result.ok = true;
    result.preview = looks_like_stack_address(instance_ea)
      ? qstring("(stack-local, attach debugger to preview)")
      : qstring("(unable to read memory)");
    return result;
  }

  ea_t head = BADADDR;
  uint64 size = 0;
  std::memcpy(&head, header.data.begin() + MYHEAD_OFFSET, sizeof(ea_t));
  std::memcpy(&size, header.data.begin() + MYSIZE_OFFSET, sizeof(size));

  result.from_live_memory = header.from_debugger;
  result.ok = true;
  result.preview.sprnt(
    "count=%" FMT_64 "u root=%a (header only, node traversal not supported in v1)",
    uint64(size),
    head);
  return result;
}

const stl_type_descriptor_t TREE_DESCRIPTOR =
{
  "std_tree_header", "std::map/set (header only, v1 stub)",
  &matches_impl, &build_or_get_type_impl, &decode_preview_impl
};

} // namespace

void register_stl_tree_descriptors(std::vector<const stl_type_descriptor_t *> *out)
{
  out->push_back(&TREE_DESCRIPTOR);
}

} // namespace stl_utility

```

`stl-utility/src/stl_unique_ptr.cpp`:

```cpp
// MSVC std::unique_ptr<T> descriptor (default_delete<T> case). With a
// stateless deleter, empty-base-class-optimization collapses the
// _Compressed_pair<Deleter,T*> down to a single 8-byte T* at offset 0 --
// so the applied type is simply "T *", no synthetic struct needed.
// (A stateful deleter changes this layout; out of scope for v1.)

#include "stl_registry.h"
#include "stl_memory.h"
#include "stl_util.h"

#include <kernwin.hpp>
#include <name.hpp>
#include <typeinf.hpp>

#include <cctype>
#include <cstring>
#include <string>

namespace stl_utility
{

namespace
{

std::string extract_element_type_name(const qstring *hint)
{
  if ( hint == nullptr )
    return {};

  const std::string text(hint->c_str());
  const size_t marker = text.find("unique_ptr<");
  if ( marker == std::string::npos )
    return {};

  const size_t args_start = marker + std::strlen("unique_ptr<");
  int depth = 1;
  size_t cursor = args_start;
  size_t first_comma = std::string::npos;
  for ( ; cursor < text.size() && depth > 0; ++cursor )
  {
    if ( text[cursor] == '<' )
      ++depth;
    else if ( text[cursor] == '>' )
      --depth;
    else if ( text[cursor] == ',' && depth == 1 && first_comma == std::string::npos )
      first_comma = cursor;
  }
  if ( depth != 0 )
    return {};

  const size_t args_end = first_comma != std::string::npos ? first_comma : cursor - 1;
  if ( args_end <= args_start )
    return {};

  std::string element = text.substr(args_start, args_end - args_start);
  size_t begin = 0;
  size_t end = element.size();
  while ( begin < end && std::isspace(static_cast<unsigned char>(element[begin])) != 0 )
    ++begin;
  while ( end > begin && std::isspace(static_cast<unsigned char>(element[end - 1])) != 0 )
    --end;
  return element.substr(begin, end - begin);
}

bool resolve_element_type(
      const tinfo_t *existing_type,
      const qstring *demangled_name_hint,
      tinfo_t *out_element)
{
  if ( existing_type != nullptr && !existing_type->empty() && existing_type->is_ptr() )
  {
    tinfo_t pointee = existing_type->get_pointed_object();
    if ( !pointee.empty() )
    {
      *out_element = pointee;
      return true;
    }
  }

  const std::string extracted = extract_element_type_name(demangled_name_hint);
  if ( !extracted.empty() && parse_type_string(extracted.c_str(), out_element) )
    return true;

  qstring typed;
  if ( ask_str(&typed, HIST_TYPE, "std::unique_ptr element type (e.g. \"int\", \"MyStruct *\"):")
    && parse_type_string(typed.c_str(), out_element) )
  {
    return true;
  }

  return false;
}

bool build_or_get_type_impl(
      til_t *,
      const tinfo_t *existing_type,
      const qstring *demangled_name_hint,
      tinfo_t *out_type,
      qstring *out_name)
{
  tinfo_t element;
  if ( !resolve_element_type(existing_type, demangled_name_hint, &element) )
  {
    debug_log("stl_unique_ptr: could not resolve element type, aborting retype");
    return false;
  }

  if ( !out_type->create_ptr(element) )
    return false;

  *out_name = printable_type_name(*out_type);
  return true;
}

match_confidence_t matches_impl(const tinfo_t *existing_type, const qstring *demangled_name_hint)
{
  if ( demangled_name_hint != nullptr && demangled_name_hint->find("unique_ptr<") != qstring::npos )
    return MATCH_HIGH;

  // A bare pointer is too common/ambiguous to guess as unique_ptr on its
  // own -- only match when the name explicitly says so.
  (void)existing_type;
  return MATCH_NONE;
}

stl_decode_result_t decode_preview_impl(ea_t instance_ea, const tinfo_t &)
{
  stl_decode_result_t result;

  if ( instance_ea == BADADDR )
  {
    result.ok = true;
    result.preview = "(no fixed address)";
    return result;
  }

  const stl_read_result_t header = stl_read_memory(instance_ea, sizeof(ea_t));
  if ( !header.ok )
  {
    result.ok = true;
    result.preview = looks_like_stack_address(instance_ea)
      ? qstring("(stack-local, attach debugger to preview)")
      : qstring("(unable to read memory)");
    return result;
  }

  ea_t target = BADADDR;
  std::memcpy(&target, header.data.begin(), sizeof(ea_t));
  result.from_live_memory = header.from_debugger;
  result.ok = true;

  if ( target == BADADDR || target == 0 )
  {
    result.preview = "empty";
    return result;
  }

  qstring name = get_name(target);
  if ( !name.empty() )
    result.preview.sprnt("-> %a (%s)", target, name.c_str());
  else
    result.preview.sprnt("-> %a", target);
  return result;
}

const stl_type_descriptor_t UNIQUE_PTR_DESCRIPTOR =
{
  "std_unique_ptr", "std::unique_ptr<T>",
  &matches_impl, &build_or_get_type_impl, &decode_preview_impl
};

} // namespace

void register_stl_unique_ptr_descriptors(std::vector<const stl_type_descriptor_t *> *out)
{
  out->push_back(&UNIQUE_PTR_DESCRIPTOR);
}

} // namespace stl_utility

```

`stl-utility/src/stl_unordered_map.cpp`:

```cpp
// MSVC std::unordered_map/std::unordered_set descriptor -- v1 STUB, header
// stats only. Both containers share the same underlying `_Hash<_Traits>`
// layout (only the value_type differs), so this is ONE descriptor covering
// both, mirroring src/stl_tree.cpp's precedent of covering
// map/set/multimap/multiset with a single descriptor.
//
// Layout derived from the REAL MSVC STL header source (confirmed by reading
// it directly, not just trusting a summary):
//   C:\Program Files\Microsoft Visual Studio\2022\Community\VC\Tools\MSVC\
//   14.44.35207\include\xhash, class `_Hash`, around line 2009-2019:
//
//   class _Hash {
//     _Traits _Traitsobj;    // _Umap_traits -> _Uhash_compare ->
//                            // _Compressed_pair<Hasher, _Compressed_pair<
//                            // Keyeq, float>>. For the common case
//                            // (stateless std::hash<K>/std::equal_to<K>),
//                            // EBO collapses this down to JUST the trailing
//                            // `float` (max_load_factor, default 1.0f) --
//                            // everything else is zero-sized.
//     _Mylist _List;          // = std::list<pair<const Kty,Ty>, Alloc> --
//                            // a REAL embedded std::list object:
//                            // {_Myhead (node ptr), _Mysize (size_t)} for a
//                            // stateless allocator (standard list layout).
//     _Hash_vec<_Aliter> _Vec; // vector-shaped bucket array:
//                            // {_Myfirst,_Mylast,_Myend} pointers (standard
//                            // vector layout, Release/_ITERATOR_DEBUG_LEVEL
//                            // =0 build, no _Myproxy field); each element a
//                            // pair<node*,node*> (16 bytes) denoting one
//                            // bucket's [begin,end) range.
//     size_type _Mask;        // bucket_count - 1
//     size_type _Maxidx;      // current max bucket count, power of 2
//   };
//
// The ONE uncertain detail was the exact padding after the leading `float`
// in `_Traitsobj` before `_List` starts (which needs 8-byte alignment for
// its pointer member). Standard-layout C++ rules mean the compiler lays out
// `_Traitsobj` (just a 4-byte float after EBO) at offset 0, then pads to the
// next 8-byte boundary before `_List` begins at offset 8:
//
//   offset 0x00: float    _MaxLoadFactor   (4 bytes; 4 bytes trailing pad)
//   offset 0x08: void*    _List_Myhead
//   offset 0x10: size_t   _List_Mysize
//   offset 0x18: void*    _Vec_Myfirst     (bucket-pair array begin)
//   offset 0x20: void*    _Vec_Mylast
//   offset 0x28: void*    _Vec_Myend
//   offset 0x30: size_t   _Mask
//   offset 0x38: size_t   _Maxidx
//   sizeof == 0x40
//
// IMPORTANT, unlike this plugin's other descriptors: this layout is
// header-derived and UNVERIFIED against a live binary. ScrapMechanic.exe
// (this plugin's usual real-world test target) was confirmed via MCP search
// to have NO real std::unordered_map/std::unordered_set usage anywhere --
// it uses Bullet Physics' own unrelated btHashMap instead -- so there is no
// decompiled ground-truth instance to verify this against. Compensate by
// being extra defensive: build_or_get_type_impl() below explicitly checks
// the built type's final get_size() against the expected 0x40 and logs a
// warning (rather than silently proceeding) if it doesn't match.
//
// Scope, matching src/stl_tree.cpp's precedent: header stats only (element
// count, bucket count, max load factor). Full linked-list node traversal
// (walking `_List`'s node chain, each node `{Next,Prev,pair<K,V>}`, to dump
// keys/values) requires live memory reads and is deferred to a v2 pass, NOT
// attempted here. A release/destructor collapse for unordered_map is also
// out of scope for this pass -- unlike vector's trivial-element shortcut,
// unordered_map's teardown is a substantially more complex per-node
// destruction loop that isn't safe to pattern-match yet.

#include "stl_registry.h"
#include "stl_memory.h"
#include "stl_util.h"

#include <typeinf.hpp>

#include <cstring>

namespace stl_utility
{

namespace
{

constexpr size_t STRUCT_SIZE = 0x40;
constexpr size_t MAXLOADFACTOR_OFFSET = 0x00;
constexpr size_t LIST_MYHEAD_OFFSET = 0x08;
constexpr size_t LIST_MYSIZE_OFFSET = 0x10;
constexpr size_t VEC_MYFIRST_OFFSET = 0x18;
constexpr size_t VEC_MYLAST_OFFSET = 0x20;
constexpr size_t VEC_MYEND_OFFSET = 0x28;
constexpr size_t MASK_OFFSET = 0x30;
constexpr size_t MAXIDX_OFFSET = 0x38;
constexpr const char *SYNTHETIC_NAME = "__stl_unordered_map_header";

bool build_or_get_type_impl(til_t *, const tinfo_t *, const qstring *, tinfo_t *out_type, qstring *out_name)
{
  tinfo_t existing;
  if ( existing.get_named_type(nullptr, SYNTHETIC_NAME, BTF_STRUCT) && existing.get_size() == STRUCT_SIZE )
  {
    *out_type = existing;
    *out_name = SYNTHETIC_NAME;
    return true;
  }

  tinfo_t float_type, void_type, void_ptr, size_type, pad_type;
  if ( !float_type.create_simple_type(BTF_FLOAT)
    || !void_type.create_simple_type(BTF_VOID)
    || !void_ptr.create_ptr(void_type)
    || !size_type.create_simple_type(BTF_UINT64)
    || !pad_type.create_simple_type(BTF_UINT32) )
  {
    return false;
  }

  udt_type_data_t p;
  p.is_union = false;
  p.add_member("_MaxLoadFactor", float_type, MAXLOADFACTOR_OFFSET * 8);
  // Explicit anonymous padding member rather than relying on inferred
  // alignment -- an explicit field is safer than trusting create_udt()'s
  // padding inference given the "one uncertain detail" this whole layout
  // hinges on (see the file-level comment above).
  p.add_member("_Pad0", pad_type, 0x04 * 8);
  p.add_member("_List_Myhead", void_ptr, LIST_MYHEAD_OFFSET * 8);
  p.add_member("_List_Mysize", size_type, LIST_MYSIZE_OFFSET * 8);
  // Bucket-array elements (pair<node*,node*>) are an opaque internal type
  // not useful to expose as a real pointer-to-something -- void* instead.
  p.add_member("_Vec_Myfirst", void_ptr, VEC_MYFIRST_OFFSET * 8);
  p.add_member("_Vec_Mylast", void_ptr, VEC_MYLAST_OFFSET * 8);
  p.add_member("_Vec_Myend", void_ptr, VEC_MYEND_OFFSET * 8);
  p.add_member("_Mask", size_type, MASK_OFFSET * 8);
  p.add_member("_Maxidx", size_type, MAXIDX_OFFSET * 8);

  tinfo_t built;
  if ( !built.create_udt(p) )
    return false;

  debug_log("stl_unordered_map: building synthetic header type '%s'", SYNTHETIC_NAME);
  if ( built.set_named_type(nullptr, SYNTHETIC_NAME, NTF_REPLACE) != TERR_OK )
    return false;

  tinfo_t saved;
  const bool have_saved = saved.get_named_type(nullptr, SYNTHETIC_NAME, BTF_STRUCT);
  const tinfo_t &final_type = have_saved ? saved : built;

  // This layout is header-derived and unverified against a live binary (see
  // file-level comment): make any padding/alignment mistake immediately
  // visible in the log rather than silently corrupting previews.
  if ( final_type.get_size() != STRUCT_SIZE )
  {
    debug_log(
      "stl_unordered_map: WARNING built type '%s' has size 0x%llX, expected 0x%llX -- "
      "layout assumption may be wrong",
      SYNTHETIC_NAME,
      uint64(final_type.get_size()),
      uint64(STRUCT_SIZE));
  }

  *out_type = final_type;
  *out_name = SYNTHETIC_NAME;
  return true;
}

match_confidence_t matches_impl(const tinfo_t *existing_type, const qstring *demangled_name_hint)
{
  if ( demangled_name_hint != nullptr )
  {
    static const char *const markers[] = { "unordered_map<", "unordered_set<" };
    for ( const char *marker : markers )
    {
      if ( demangled_name_hint->find(marker) != qstring::npos )
        return MATCH_HIGH;
    }

    if ( *demangled_name_hint == SYNTHETIC_NAME )
      return MATCH_HIGH;
  }

  if ( existing_type != nullptr && !existing_type->empty() && existing_type->get_size() == STRUCT_SIZE )
    return MATCH_AMBIGUOUS;

  return MATCH_NONE;
}

stl_decode_result_t decode_preview_impl(ea_t instance_ea, const tinfo_t &)
{
  stl_decode_result_t result;

  if ( instance_ea == BADADDR )
  {
    result.ok = true;
    result.preview = "(no fixed address)";
    return result;
  }

  const stl_read_result_t header = stl_read_memory(instance_ea, STRUCT_SIZE);
  if ( !header.ok )
  {
    result.ok = true;
    result.preview = looks_like_stack_address(instance_ea)
      ? qstring("(stack-local, attach debugger to preview)")
      : qstring("(unable to read memory)");
    return result;
  }

  float max_load_factor = 0.0f;
  uint64 list_size = 0;
  uint64 mask = 0;
  std::memcpy(&max_load_factor, header.data.begin() + MAXLOADFACTOR_OFFSET, sizeof(max_load_factor));
  std::memcpy(&list_size, header.data.begin() + LIST_MYSIZE_OFFSET, sizeof(list_size));
  std::memcpy(&mask, header.data.begin() + MASK_OFFSET, sizeof(mask));

  result.from_live_memory = header.from_debugger;
  result.ok = true;
  result.preview.sprnt(
    "size=%" FMT_64 "u buckets=%" FMT_64 "u max_load_factor=%.2f "
    "(header only, node traversal not supported)",
    list_size,
    mask + 1,
    double(max_load_factor));
  return result;
}

const stl_type_descriptor_t UNORDERED_MAP_DESCRIPTOR =
{
  "std_unordered_map_header", "std::unordered_map/unordered_set (header only, v1 stub)",
  &matches_impl, &build_or_get_type_impl, &decode_preview_impl
};

} // namespace

void register_stl_unordered_map_descriptors(std::vector<const stl_type_descriptor_t *> *out)
{
  out->push_back(&UNORDERED_MAP_DESCRIPTOR);
}

} // namespace stl_utility
```

`stl-utility/src/stl_util.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "stl_util.h"

#include <diskio.hpp>
#include <fpro.h>

#include <cctype>
#include <cstdarg>

namespace stl_utility
{

void debug_log(const char *format, ...)
{
  qstring path;
  path.sprnt("%s/IDA-STL-Utility.log", get_user_idadir());
  FILE *file = qfopen(path.c_str(), "a");
  if ( file == nullptr )
    return;

  qstring message;
  va_list args;
  va_start(args, format);
  message.vsprnt(format, args);
  va_end(args);
  qfprintf(
    file,
    "[%u.%06u] %s\n",
    get_secs(qtime64()),
    get_usecs(qtime64()),
    message.c_str());
  qfclose(file);
}

std::string trimmed_plain_text(const qstring &tagged)
{
  qstring plain_qstr;
  tag_remove(&plain_qstr, tagged);
  std::string plain(plain_qstr.c_str());
  size_t begin = 0;
  size_t end = plain.size();
  while ( begin < end && std::isspace(static_cast<unsigned char>(plain[begin])) != 0 )
    ++begin;
  while ( end > begin && std::isspace(static_cast<unsigned char>(plain[end - 1])) != 0 )
    --end;
  return plain.substr(begin, end - begin);
}

qstring printable_type_name(const tinfo_t &type)
{
  qstring out;
  if ( type.empty() )
    return out;
  type.print(&out);
  return out;
}

bool parse_type_string(const char *decl, tinfo_t *out_type)
{
  if ( decl == nullptr || *decl == '\0' )
    return false;

  std::string with_semicolon(decl);
  size_t end = with_semicolon.size();
  while ( end > 0 && std::isspace(static_cast<unsigned char>(with_semicolon[end - 1])) != 0 )
    --end;
  with_semicolon.erase(end);
  if ( with_semicolon.empty() )
    return false;
  if ( with_semicolon.back() != ';' )
    with_semicolon.push_back(';');

  qstring name;
  return parse_decl(out_type, &name, nullptr, with_semicolon.c_str(), PT_TYP | PT_SIL)
      && !out_type->empty();
}

} // namespace stl_utility

```

`stl-utility/src/stl_util.h`:

```h
#pragma once

#include <hexrays.hpp>

#include <string>

namespace stl_utility
{

// Writes to %APPDATA%\Hex-Rays\IDA Pro\IDA-STL-Utility.log, mirroring the
// sister IDA-VTable-Utility plugin's debug_log convention.
void debug_log(const char *format, ...);

// Strips color tags and surrounding whitespace from a Hex-Rays-rendered
// string (as produced by cexpr_t::print1/cfunc_t line text).
std::string trimmed_plain_text(const qstring &tagged);

// Returns the demangled/printable type name for a tinfo_t (e.g.
// "std::basic_string<char,std::char_traits<char>,std::allocator<char>>"),
// or an empty string if the type is empty/unprintable.
qstring printable_type_name(const tinfo_t &type);

// Parses a single C type declaration typed by the user (e.g. "int",
// "MyStruct *") into a tinfo_t. Appends the trailing ';' parse_decl expects
// if the caller didn't include one.
bool parse_type_string(const char *decl, tinfo_t *out_type);

} // namespace stl_utility
```
