`xxhash-utility/src/xxhash32_match.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "xxhash_match.h"

namespace xxhash_utility
{

bool try_match_xxh32(cinsn_t *outer_if, xxhash_match_t *out)
{
  match_orchestration_result_t result;
  if ( !match_xxhash_pattern(outer_if, false, &result) )
    return false;

  out->matched_stmt = result.matched_stmt;
  out->variant = XXHASH_32;
  out->input = result.input;
  out->seed_expr = result.seed_expr;
  out->hash_target = result.hash_target;
  return true;
}

} // namespace xxhash_utility

```

`xxhash-utility/src/xxhash64_match.cpp`:

```cpp
// XXH64 support is preliminary: it reuses the exact same orchestration as
// XXH32 (loop shape, convergence, small/large split, finalize-tail call),
// parameterized for 64-bit primes/rotate-31/advance-32, but does NOT yet
// recognize the extra per-accumulator merge-round pass XXH64 performs
// before convergence (see the plan doc's algorithm notes). Real-world
// XXH64 call sites that include that merge-round pass will not match
// until that's implemented; flagged here and in the README as a known
// v1 limitation, since no real XXH64 sample was available to validate
// against during initial implementation.

#include "xxhash_match.h"

namespace xxhash_utility
{

bool try_match_xxh64(cinsn_t *outer_if, xxhash_match_t *out)
{
  match_orchestration_result_t result;
  if ( !match_xxhash_pattern(outer_if, true, &result) )
    return false;

  out->matched_stmt = result.matched_stmt;
  out->variant = XXHASH_64;
  out->input = result.input;
  out->seed_expr = result.seed_expr;
  out->hash_target = result.hash_target;
  return true;
}

} // namespace xxhash_utility

```

`xxhash-utility/src/xxhash_common.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "xxhash_common.h"
#include "xxhash_input.h"

#include <name.hpp>

#include <cctype>
#include <cstring>
#include <string>

namespace xxhash_utility
{

const uint32 XXH32_PRIME[6] =
{
  0,
  0x9E3779B1u, // PRIME32_1
  0x85EBCA77u, // PRIME32_2
  0xC2B2AE3Du, // PRIME32_3
  0x27D4EB2Fu, // PRIME32_4
  0x165667B1u, // PRIME32_5
};

const uint64 XXH64_PRIME[6] =
{
  0,
  0x9E3779B185EBCA87ull, // PRIME64_1
  0xC2B2AE3D27D4EB4Full, // PRIME64_2
  0x165667B19E3779F9ull, // PRIME64_3
  0x85EBCA77C2B2AE63ull, // PRIME64_4
  0x27D4EB2F165667C5ull, // PRIME64_5
};

bool is_num32(const cexpr_t *e, uint32 value)
{
  return e != nullptr && e->op == cot_num && uint32(e->numval()) == value;
}

bool is_num64(const cexpr_t *e, uint64 value)
{
  return e != nullptr && e->op == cot_num && uint64(e->numval()) == value;
}

const cexpr_t *strip_casts(const cexpr_t *e)
{
  while ( e != nullptr && e->op == cot_cast )
    e = e->x;
  return e;
}

cexpr_t *strip_casts(cexpr_t *e)
{
  while ( e != nullptr && e->op == cot_cast )
    e = e->x;
  return e;
}

bool match_rol(const cexpr_t *e, bool is_64, const cexpr_t **value, int *amount)
{
  e = strip_casts(e);
  if ( e == nullptr || e->op != cot_call || e->a == nullptr || e->a->size() != 2 )
    return false;

  const cexpr_t *callee = strip_casts(e->x);
  if ( callee == nullptr || callee->op != cot_helper || callee->helper == nullptr )
    return false;

  const char *expect = is_64 ? "__ROL8__" : "__ROL4__";
  if ( std::strcmp(callee->helper, expect) != 0 )
    return false;

  const cexpr_t *amount_expr = strip_casts(&e->a->at(1));
  if ( amount_expr == nullptr || amount_expr->op != cot_num )
    return false;

  *value = &e->a->at(0);
  *amount = int(amount_expr->numval());
  return true;
}

match_identity_t identify(const cexpr_t *e)
{
  match_identity_t result;
  e = strip_casts(e);
  if ( e == nullptr )
    return result;

  if ( e->op == cot_var )
  {
    result.is_var = true;
    result.var_idx = e->v.idx;
  }
  else if ( e->op == cot_obj )
  {
    result.is_obj = true;
    result.obj_ea = e->obj_ea;
  }
  return result;
}

// True if `parent_type` is a union UDT; on success *udt is filled with its
// details. Byte-offset-vs-member-index semantics for `m` hinge on this
// (see resolve_member_access()'s comment and the member_access_t doc
// comment in xxhash_common.h).
static bool get_union_details(const tinfo_t &parent_type, udt_type_data_t *udt)
{
  return parent_type.is_udt() && parent_type.get_udt_details(udt) && udt->is_union;
}

member_access_t resolve_member_access(const cexpr_t *e)
{
  member_access_t result;
  uint64 offset = 0;
  const cexpr_t *cur = strip_casts(e);
  bool is_leaf = true; // true only for the outermost node (the original `e`)
  while ( cur != nullptr && (cur->op == cot_memref || cur->op == cot_memptr) )
  {
    const cexpr_t *parent = strip_casts(cur->x);

    tinfo_t parent_type;
    bool have_parent_type = false;
    if ( parent != nullptr )
    {
      parent_type = parent->type;
      if ( cur->op == cot_memptr && parent_type.is_ptr() )
        parent_type = parent_type.get_pointed_object();
      have_parent_type = true;
    }

    udt_type_data_t udt;
    if ( have_parent_type && get_union_details(parent_type, &udt) )
    {
      // Union layer: `cur->m` is the member's ordinal index, not a byte
      // offset. All union members share byte offset 0, so this layer
      // contributes nothing to the cumulative offset. Record the accessed
      // member's name for the leaf access, so callers that need to
      // disambiguate which union arm was touched (offset alone can't) have
      // something to check.
      if ( is_leaf && cur->m < udt.size() )
        result.member_name = udt[cur->m].name;
    }
    else
    {
      // Plain struct (or unresolved type -- fall back to the historical
      // byte-offset assumption): `cur->m` is a byte offset.
      offset += cur->m;
    }

    is_leaf = false;
    cur = parent;
  }
  if ( cur == nullptr )
    return result;

  const match_identity_t base = identify(cur);
  if ( !base.valid() )
    return result;

  result.ok = true;
  result.base = base;
  result.offset = offset;
  return result;
}

namespace
{

// target += advance
//
// Hex-Rays represents `p += k` two different ways depending on context:
//   - cot_asgadd, where x/y hold the target and addend DIRECTLY
//     (see hexrays.hpp: "cot_asgadd = 6, ///< x += y" -- same x/y-field
//     convention as cot_asg's "x = y"), i.e. NOT y = cot_add(x, k).
//     This is what a genuine `p_boneName += 16;` source statement compiles
//     to, and is the form this matcher needs to recognize.
//   - cot_asg wrapping a cot_add on the right (`x = x + k`), which some
//     other maturity levels/compilers may still emit. Kept as a fallback
//     so this function stays correct for both encodings.
bool try_match_advance(const cexpr_t *stmt_expr, uint64 advance, match_identity_t *target)
{
  if ( stmt_expr == nullptr )
    return false;

  if ( stmt_expr->op == cot_asgadd )
  {
    const match_identity_t candidate = identify(stmt_expr->x);
    if ( !candidate.valid() )
      return false;
    if ( !is_num64(strip_casts(stmt_expr->y), advance) )
      return false;
    *target = candidate;
    return true;
  }

  if ( stmt_expr->op != cot_asg )
    return false;

  const match_identity_t candidate = identify(stmt_expr->x);
  if ( !candidate.valid() )
    return false;

  const cexpr_t *src = strip_casts(stmt_expr->y);
  if ( src == nullptr || src->op != cot_add )
    return false;

  const cexpr_t *a = strip_casts(src->x);
  const cexpr_t *b = strip_casts(src->y);
  const match_identity_t ia = identify(a);
  const match_identity_t ib = identify(b);

  if ( ia.valid() && ia == candidate && is_num64(b, advance) )
  {
    *target = candidate;
    return true;
  }
  if ( ib.valid() && ib == candidate && is_num64(a, advance) )
  {
    *target = candidate;
    return true;
  }
  return false;
}

// target = PRIME1 * rol(target (+/-) PRIME2 * *(ptr + k), rotate_amount)
bool try_match_lane(
      const cexpr_t *asg,
      bool is_64,
      int rotate_amount,
      match_identity_t *lane_target,
      match_identity_t *deref_base,
      uint64 *elem_offset)
{
  const match_identity_t target = identify(asg->x);
  if ( !target.valid() )
    return false;

  const uint64 prime1 = is_64 ? XXH64_PRIME[1] : XXH32_PRIME[1];
  const uint64 prime2 = is_64 ? XXH64_PRIME[2] : XXH32_PRIME[2];
  auto is_prime1 = [&](const cexpr_t *e) { return is_64 ? is_num64(e, prime1) : is_num32(e, uint32(prime1)); };

  // `negated` selects between the two ways the compiler can encode
  // `target + PRIME2 * deref`:
  //   - cot_add, constant == PRIME2                       (negated == false)
  //   - cot_sub, constant == two's-complement of -PRIME2  (negated == true)
  // (`target - (-PRIME2) * deref` === `target + PRIME2 * deref` mod 2^N,
  // and the compiler is free to fold the sign into the constant instead of
  // re-emitting cot_add with the positive literal.)
  auto is_prime2 = [&](const cexpr_t *e, bool negated)
  {
    if ( is_64 )
    {
      const uint64 want = negated ? uint64(0) - prime2 : prime2;
      return is_num64(e, want);
    }
    const uint32 want = negated ? uint32(0) - uint32(prime2) : uint32(prime2);
    return is_num32(e, want);
  };

  const cexpr_t *src = strip_casts(asg->y);
  if ( src == nullptr || src->op != cot_mul )
    return false;

  const cexpr_t *a = strip_casts(src->x);
  const cexpr_t *b = strip_casts(src->y);
  const cexpr_t *rol_candidate = nullptr;
  if ( is_prime1(a) )
    rol_candidate = b;
  else if ( is_prime1(b) )
    rol_candidate = a;
  if ( rol_candidate == nullptr )
    return false;

  const cexpr_t *rotated_value = nullptr;
  int amount = 0;
  if ( !match_rol(rol_candidate, is_64, &rotated_value, &amount) || amount != rotate_amount )
    return false;

  const cexpr_t *inner = strip_casts(rotated_value);
  if ( inner == nullptr || (inner->op != cot_add && inner->op != cot_sub) )
    return false;

  const cexpr_t *ia_expr = strip_casts(inner->x);
  const cexpr_t *ib_expr = strip_casts(inner->y);
  match_identity_t self_id = identify(ia_expr);
  const cexpr_t *mul_expr = ib_expr;
  bool self_on_left = true;
  if ( !(self_id.valid() && self_id == target) )
  {
    self_id = identify(ib_expr);
    mul_expr = ia_expr;
    self_on_left = false;
  }
  if ( !(self_id.valid() && self_id == target) || mul_expr == nullptr || mul_expr->op != cot_mul )
    return false;

  // `PRIME2 * deref - target` is not an equivalent form of the round (it's
  // sign-inverted relative to `target (+ / negated-sub) PRIME2 * deref`), so
  // only accept cot_sub when the target is the left-hand operand.
  if ( inner->op == cot_sub && !self_on_left )
    return false;
  const bool sub_negated_prime2 = inner->op == cot_sub;

  const cexpr_t *ma = strip_casts(mul_expr->x);
  const cexpr_t *mb = strip_casts(mul_expr->y);
  const cexpr_t *deref_expr = nullptr;
  if ( is_prime2(ma, sub_negated_prime2) )
    deref_expr = mb;
  else if ( is_prime2(mb, sub_negated_prime2) )
    deref_expr = ma;
  if ( deref_expr == nullptr )
    return false;

  deref_expr = strip_casts(deref_expr);
  if ( deref_expr == nullptr || deref_expr->op != cot_ptr )
    return false;

  const cexpr_t *base = strip_casts(deref_expr->x);
  if ( base == nullptr )
    return false;

  match_identity_t base_id;
  uint64 offset = 0;
  if ( base->op == cot_add )
  {
    const cexpr_t *ba = strip_casts(base->x);
    const cexpr_t *bb = strip_casts(base->y);
    const match_identity_t bia = identify(ba);
    const match_identity_t bib = identify(bb);
    if ( bia.valid() && bb != nullptr && bb->op == cot_num )
    {
      base_id = bia;
      offset = bb->numval();
    }
    else if ( bib.valid() && ba != nullptr && ba->op == cot_num )
    {
      base_id = bib;
      offset = ba->numval();
    }
    else
    {
      return false;
    }
  }
  else
  {
    base_id = identify(base);
    offset = 0;
  }
  if ( !base_id.valid() )
    return false;

  *lane_target = target;
  *deref_base = base_id;
  *elem_offset = offset;
  return true;
}

} // namespace

bool match_lane_loop(const cinsn_t *do_stmt, bool is_64, lane_loop_match_t *out)
{
  if ( do_stmt == nullptr || do_stmt->op != cit_do || do_stmt->cdo == nullptr )
    return false;

  cinsn_t *body = do_stmt->cdo->body;
  if ( body == nullptr || body->op != cit_block || body->cblock == nullptr )
    return false;

  const uint64 advance = is_64 ? 32 : 16;
  const int rotate_amount = is_64 ? 31 : 13;

  match_identity_t lanes[4];
  bool lane_found[4] = { false, false, false, false };
  match_identity_t ptr_var;
  bool ptr_var_found = false;
  bool advance_found = false;

  for ( cinsn_t &stmt : *body->cblock )
  {
    if ( stmt.op != cit_expr || stmt.cexpr == nullptr )
      return false;

    // The pointer-advance statement (`p += 16/32;`) is a genuine compound
    // assignment and Hex-Rays represents it as cot_asgadd, not as cot_asg
    // wrapping a cot_add -- see the comment on try_match_advance. The lane
    // updates (`v10 = PRIME1 * rol(...);`) are plain reassignments, always
    // cot_asg. Accept either opcode here, then let the two try_match_*
    // helpers below do the strict, role-specific classification.
    const ctype_t top_op = stmt.cexpr->op;
    if ( top_op != cot_asg && top_op != cot_asgadd )
      return false;

    match_identity_t advance_target;
    if ( try_match_advance(stmt.cexpr, advance, &advance_target) )
    {
      if ( advance_found )
        return false; // strict: exactly one pointer-advance statement
      ptr_var = advance_target;
      ptr_var_found = true;
      advance_found = true;
      continue;
    }

    // A cot_asgadd statement that try_match_advance rejected can only be
    // some other compound assignment -- not a lane update (those are
    // cot_asg) -- so reject rather than falling through to try_match_lane.
    if ( top_op != cot_asg )
      return false;

    match_identity_t lane_target;
    match_identity_t deref_base;
    uint64 elem_offset = 0;
    if ( !try_match_lane(stmt.cexpr, is_64, rotate_amount, &lane_target, &deref_base, &elem_offset) )
      return false; // strict: every statement must be classified

    if ( elem_offset > 3 || lane_found[elem_offset] )
      return false;
    if ( ptr_var_found && !(ptr_var == deref_base) )
      return false;

    if ( !ptr_var_found )
    {
      ptr_var = deref_base;
      ptr_var_found = true;
    }
    lanes[elem_offset] = lane_target;
    lane_found[elem_offset] = true;
  }

  if ( !advance_found || !ptr_var_found )
    return false;
  for ( int i = 0; i < 4; ++i )
  {
    if ( !lane_found[i] )
      return false;
  }

  out->ptr_var = ptr_var;
  for ( int i = 0; i < 4; ++i )
    out->lanes[i] = lanes[i];
  return true;
}

namespace
{

void collect_add_chain(const cexpr_t *e, qvector<const cexpr_t *> *out)
{
  e = strip_casts(e);
  if ( e == nullptr )
    return;
  if ( e->op == cot_add )
  {
    collect_add_chain(e->x, out);
    collect_add_chain(e->y, out);
  }
  else
  {
    out->push_back(e);
  }
}

} // namespace

bool match_convergence(
      const cinsn_t *stmt,
      bool is_64,
      const match_identity_t lanes[4],
      match_identity_t *target_out)
{
  if ( stmt == nullptr || stmt->op != cit_expr || stmt->cexpr == nullptr || stmt->cexpr->op != cot_asg )
    return false;

  const match_identity_t target = identify(stmt->cexpr->x);
  if ( !target.valid() )
    return false;

  qvector<const cexpr_t *> addends;
  collect_add_chain(stmt->cexpr->y, &addends);
  if ( addends.size() != 4 )
    return false;

  static const int expected_amounts[4] = { 1, 7, 12, 18 };
  bool amount_used[4] = { false, false, false, false };
  bool lane_used[4] = { false, false, false, false };

  for ( const cexpr_t *addend : addends )
  {
    const cexpr_t *value = nullptr;
    int amount = 0;
    if ( !match_rol(addend, is_64, &value, &amount) )
      return false;

    int amount_slot = -1;
    for ( int i = 0; i < 4; ++i )
    {
      if ( !amount_used[i] && expected_amounts[i] == amount )
      {
        amount_slot = i;
        break;
      }
    }
    if ( amount_slot < 0 )
      return false;

    const match_identity_t value_id = identify(value);
    int lane_slot = -1;
    for ( int i = 0; i < 4; ++i )
    {
      if ( !lane_used[i] && value_id.valid() && value_id == lanes[i] )
      {
        lane_slot = i;
        break;
      }
    }
    if ( lane_slot < 0 )
      return false;

    amount_used[amount_slot] = true;
    lane_used[lane_slot] = true;
  }

  *target_out = target;
  return true;
}

namespace
{

bool assigns_target(const cinsn_t *stmt, match_identity_t target)
{
  if ( stmt == nullptr || stmt->op != cit_expr || stmt->cexpr == nullptr || stmt->cexpr->op != cot_asg )
    return false;
  const match_identity_t candidate = identify(stmt->cexpr->x);
  return candidate.valid() && candidate == target;
}

// Handles both a curly-braced branch (cit_block) and a single-statement
// branch (Hex-Rays sometimes omits the block for a one-line branch).
bool block_or_single_assigns(cinsn_t *branch, match_identity_t target)
{
  if ( branch == nullptr )
    return false;
  if ( branch->op != cit_block )
    return assigns_target(branch, target);

  bool found = false;
  for_each_top_level_stmt(branch, [&](cinsn_t *stmt)
  {
    if ( assigns_target(stmt, target) )
      found = true;
  });
  return found;
}

cinsn_t *find_top_level_do(cinsn_t *branch)
{
  if ( branch == nullptr )
    return nullptr;
  if ( branch->op == cit_do )
    return branch;
  if ( branch->op != cit_block )
    return nullptr;

  cinsn_t *found = nullptr;
  for_each_top_level_stmt(branch, [&](cinsn_t *stmt)
  {
    if ( stmt->op == cit_do )
      found = stmt;
  });
  return found;
}

bool looks_like_finalize_name(ea_t ea)
{
  if ( ea == BADADDR )
    return false;
  qstring name = get_name(ea);
  std::string lowered(name.c_str());
  for ( char &c : lowered )
    c = char(std::tolower(static_cast<unsigned char>(c)));
  return lowered.find("finaliz") != std::string::npos;
}

// Recognizes the finalization-tail call: `target = Callee(arg0, ptr, remaining_len, seed);`
// Accepts any callee name if it structurally receives `ptr_var` as one of
// its arguments (since the callee may just be an unnamed sub_XXXXXX);
// otherwise requires the name to look like a "finalize"-shaped helper.
bool match_tail_call(
      const cinsn_t *stmt,
      match_identity_t ptr_var,
      match_identity_t *hash_target_out,
      const cexpr_t **seed_expr_out,
      const cexpr_t **ptr_arg_out,
      const cexpr_t **length_arg_out)
{
  if ( stmt == nullptr || stmt->op != cit_expr || stmt->cexpr == nullptr || stmt->cexpr->op != cot_asg )
    return false;

  const cexpr_t *call = strip_casts(stmt->cexpr->y);
  if ( call == nullptr || call->op != cot_call || call->a == nullptr || call->a->size() != 4 )
    return false;

  bool name_ok = false;
  const cexpr_t *callee = strip_casts(call->x);
  if ( callee != nullptr && callee->op == cot_obj )
    name_ok = looks_like_finalize_name(callee->obj_ea);

  bool structurally_ok = false;
  for ( size_t i = 0; i < call->a->size(); ++i )
  {
    const match_identity_t arg_id = identify(&call->a->at(i));
    if ( arg_id.valid() && arg_id == ptr_var )
    {
      structurally_ok = true;
      break;
    }
  }

  if ( !name_ok && !structurally_ok )
    return false;

  const match_identity_t target = identify(stmt->cexpr->x);
  if ( !target.valid() )
    return false;

  *hash_target_out = target;
  *seed_expr_out = &call->a->at(3);
  *ptr_arg_out = &call->a->at(1);
  *length_arg_out = &call->a->at(2);
  return true;
}

} // namespace

bool match_xxhash_pattern(cinsn_t *outer_if, bool is_64, match_orchestration_result_t *out)
{
  if ( outer_if == nullptr || outer_if->op != cit_if || outer_if->cif == nullptr )
    return false;

  cif_t *cif = outer_if->cif;
  if ( cif->ielse == nullptr )
    return false; // strict: the "no data -> hash=0" else branch is required

  cinsn_t *then_block = cif->ithen;
  if ( then_block == nullptr || then_block->op != cit_block )
    return false;

  // Find the small/large split: a cit_if among then_block's top-level
  // statements with a cit_do on exactly one of its branches.
  cinsn_t *inner_if = nullptr;
  cinsn_t *large_branch = nullptr;
  cinsn_t *small_branch = nullptr;
  for_each_top_level_stmt(then_block, [&](cinsn_t *stmt)
  {
    if ( inner_if != nullptr || stmt->op != cit_if || stmt->cif == nullptr )
      return;
    if ( stmt->cif->ielse == nullptr )
      return;

    cinsn_t *then_loop = find_top_level_do(stmt->cif->ithen);
    cinsn_t *else_loop = find_top_level_do(stmt->cif->ielse);
    if ( (then_loop != nullptr) == (else_loop != nullptr) )
      return; // require exactly one side to hold the loop

    inner_if = stmt;
    if ( then_loop != nullptr )
    {
      large_branch = stmt->cif->ithen;
      small_branch = stmt->cif->ielse;
    }
    else
    {
      large_branch = stmt->cif->ielse;
      small_branch = stmt->cif->ithen;
    }
  });
  if ( inner_if == nullptr )
    return false;

  cinsn_t *do_stmt = find_top_level_do(large_branch);
  if ( do_stmt == nullptr )
  {
    debug_log("xxhash%d @ %a: no do-loop found in large branch", is_64 ? 64 : 32, outer_if->ea);
    return false;
  }

  lane_loop_match_t loop_match;
  if ( !match_lane_loop(do_stmt, is_64, &loop_match) )
  {
    debug_log("xxhash%d @ %a: lane loop match failed", is_64 ? 64 : 32, outer_if->ea);
    return false;
  }

  // Light presence check: the 4 lane variables must each appear as an
  // assignment target somewhere in the large branch (the accumulator
  // seeding), without further validating the exact seeding formula --
  // real-world seed folding turned out to be more variable than the
  // textbook formula, so the loop/prime/rotate/convergence/tail matches
  // below are the actual strictness backstop.
  bool lane_seeded[4] = { false, false, false, false };
  for_each_top_level_stmt(large_branch, [&](cinsn_t *stmt)
  {
    if ( stmt->op != cit_expr || stmt->cexpr == nullptr || stmt->cexpr->op != cot_asg )
      return;
    const match_identity_t target = identify(stmt->cexpr->x);
    if ( !target.valid() )
      return;
    for ( int i = 0; i < 4; ++i )
    {
      if ( target == loop_match.lanes[i] )
        lane_seeded[i] = true;
    }
  });
  for ( int i = 0; i < 4; ++i )
  {
    if ( !lane_seeded[i] )
    {
      debug_log("xxhash%d @ %a: lane %d never seeded in large branch", is_64 ? 64 : 32, outer_if->ea, i);
      return false;
    }
  }

  // Find the convergence assignment among large_branch's top-level
  // statements (after the loop).
  match_identity_t intermediate_var;
  bool have_convergence = false;
  for_each_top_level_stmt(large_branch, [&](cinsn_t *stmt)
  {
    if ( have_convergence )
      return;
    match_identity_t target;
    if ( match_convergence(stmt, is_64, loop_match.lanes, &target) )
    {
      intermediate_var = target;
      have_convergence = true;
    }
  });
  if ( !have_convergence )
  {
    debug_log("xxhash%d @ %a: convergence not found", is_64 ? 64 : 32, outer_if->ea);
    return false;
  }

  // The small-input branch must assign the same intermediate variable.
  if ( !block_or_single_assigns(small_branch, intermediate_var) )
  {
    debug_log(
      "xxhash%d @ %a: small-input branch does not assign the intermediate variable",
      is_64 ? 64 : 32,
      outer_if->ea);
    return false;
  }

  // Find the finalization tail call: a top-level statement of then_block
  // (a sibling after inner_if) shaped like `hash = Callee(a, ptr, len, seed);`.
  match_identity_t hash_target_id;
  const cexpr_t *seed_expr = nullptr;
  const cexpr_t *ptr_arg = nullptr;
  const cexpr_t *length_arg = nullptr;
  const cexpr_t *hash_target_expr = nullptr;
  bool have_tail = false;
  for_each_top_level_stmt(then_block, [&](cinsn_t *stmt)
  {
    if ( have_tail )
      return;
    if ( match_tail_call(stmt, loop_match.ptr_var, &hash_target_id, &seed_expr, &ptr_arg, &length_arg) )
    {
      hash_target_expr = stmt->cexpr->x;
      have_tail = true;
    }
  });
  if ( !have_tail )
  {
    debug_log("xxhash%d @ %a: finalize tail call not found", is_64 ? 64 : 32, outer_if->ea);
    return false;
  }

  // Resolve the hashed input: prefer std::string SSO recognition (gives a
  // semantically clear _Mysize length expression), falling back to the
  // tail call's own pointer/length arguments for the raw-buffer case.
  xxhash_input_t input;
  if ( !try_resolve_string_input(then_block, &input) )
  {
    const cexpr_t *raw_length = length_arg;
    const cexpr_t *stripped_length = strip_casts(length_arg);
    if ( stripped_length != nullptr && stripped_length->op == cot_band && stripped_length->y != nullptr
      && stripped_length->y->op == cot_num )
    {
      raw_length = stripped_length->x;
    }
    if ( !try_resolve_raw_buffer_input(ptr_arg, raw_length, &input) )
    {
      debug_log("xxhash%d @ %a: failed to resolve hashed input", is_64 ? 64 : 32, outer_if->ea);
      return false;
    }
  }

  out->matched_stmt = outer_if;
  out->input = input;
  out->seed_expr = seed_expr;
  out->hash_target = hash_target_expr;
  return true;
}

} // namespace xxhash_utility

```

`xxhash-utility/src/xxhash_common.h`:

```h
#pragma once

#include <hexrays.hpp>

#include "xxhash_input.h"

namespace xxhash_utility
{

// Appends a timestamped line to %APPDATA%\Hex-Rays\IDA Pro\IDA-XXHash-Utility.log.
// Shared by xxhash_plugin.cpp (top-level collapse/failure logging) and
// xxhash_common.cpp (structural-rejection logging inside match_xxhash_pattern).
void debug_log(const char *format, ...);

enum xxhash_variant_t
{
  XXHASH_32,
  XXHASH_64,
};

// XXH32 primes, indexed 1..5 (index 0 unused).
extern const uint32 XXH32_PRIME[6];
// XXH64 primes, indexed 1..5 (index 0 unused).
extern const uint64 XXH64_PRIME[6];

// True if `e` is a cot_num literal equal to `value` (compared in the given
// bit width, tolerant of the signed/unsigned representation a decompiler
// may choose to print).
bool is_num32(const cexpr_t *e, uint32 value);
bool is_num64(const cexpr_t *e, uint64 value);

// Strips cot_cast wrapper(s) to reach the underlying expression.
const cexpr_t *strip_casts(const cexpr_t *e);
cexpr_t *strip_casts(cexpr_t *e);

// Recognizes a __ROL4__/__ROL8__ compiler-intrinsic helper call
// (cot_call to a cot_helper named accordingly). On success, *value is set
// to the rotated operand (arg 0) and *amount to the rotate amount, which
// must itself be a cot_num literal (arg 1).
bool match_rol(const cexpr_t *e, bool is_64, const cexpr_t **value, int *amount);

// Identifies a variable/global referenced by `e` (after stripping casts).
// Two match_identity_t values compare equal only when they refer to the
// exact same lvar (by index) or the exact same global (by address) --
// never by textual name, since compiler-generated names differ per call
// site and are not a reliable identity signal.
struct match_identity_t
{
  bool is_var = false;
  bool is_obj = false;
  int var_idx = -1;
  ea_t obj_ea = BADADDR;

  bool valid() const { return is_var || is_obj; }
  bool operator==(const match_identity_t &r) const
  {
    if ( is_var && r.is_var )
      return var_idx == r.var_idx;
    if ( is_obj && r.is_obj )
      return obj_ea == r.obj_ea;
    return false;
  }
};

match_identity_t identify(const cexpr_t *e);

// Resolves a (possibly nested) x.m/x->m member-access chain down to its
// ultimate base variable/global and the cumulative byte offset from that
// base. Used to recognize std::string's `_Mysize`/`_Myres`/`_Bx._Ptr`
// fields regardless of whether Hex-Rays represents the union access as one
// flattened memref or several nested ones.
//
// Per hexrays.hpp's cexpr_t::m doc comment: "member offset (used for
// cot_memptr, cot_memref) ... for unions, the member number" -- i.e. `m` is
// a BYTE OFFSET when the immediate parent expression's type is a plain
// struct, but an ORDINAL MEMBER INDEX when the immediate parent is a union.
// resolve_member_access() checks each layer's immediate parent type and
// only accumulates `m` into `offset` for struct layers; union layers
// contribute 0 (all union members share byte offset 0), and the union
// member's own name is captured into `member_name` for the outermost
// (leaf) access in the chain, in case a caller needs to disambiguate which
// union arm was actually accessed (offset alone can't, since every member
// of a union sits at the same offset).
struct member_access_t
{
  bool ok = false;
  match_identity_t base;
  uint64 offset = 0;
  // Name of the accessed member, set only when `e` itself (the outermost/
  // leaf node of the chain) is a member access whose immediate parent type
  // is a union; empty otherwise.
  qstring member_name;
};

member_access_t resolve_member_access(const cexpr_t *e);

// Result of matching the do-while lane-update loop shared by XXH32/XXH64:
//   do {
//     lane[i] = PRIME1 * rol(lane[i] (+/-) PRIME2 * *(ptr + i*element), rotate_amount);
//     ...
//     ptr += 16 (XXH32) / 32 (XXH64);
//   } while (...)
struct lane_loop_match_t
{
  match_identity_t lanes[4];
  match_identity_t ptr_var;
};

// Matches the lane-update loop body against `do_stmt` (must have
// op == cit_do). `is_64` selects PRIME32_1/2 + rotate 13 + advance 16 vs
// PRIME64_1/2 + rotate 31 + advance 32. Requires exactly 4 distinct lane
// updates (offsets {0,1,2,3} element-units from the same pointer variable)
// plus exactly one pointer-advance statement, with nothing left
// unclassified in the loop body (strict: no partial matches).
bool match_lane_loop(const cinsn_t *do_stmt, bool is_64, lane_loop_match_t *out);

// Matches a convergence assignment `target = rol(lane0,1)+rol(lane1,7)+rol(lane2,12)+rol(lane3,18)`
// (order-independent sum) against `stmt` (must be cit_expr wrapping a
// cot_asg). `lanes` must be the 4 identities from match_lane_loop, matched
// as a set (each used exactly once). On success, *target_out is the
// assignment's target identity.
bool match_convergence(
      const cinsn_t *stmt,
      bool is_64,
      const match_identity_t lanes[4],
      match_identity_t *target_out);

// Iterates the top-level statements of a cit_block statement, calling
// `visit(stmt)` for each. Returns false if `block_stmt` isn't a block.
template <typename Visitor>
bool for_each_top_level_stmt(cinsn_t *block_stmt, Visitor visit)
{
  if ( block_stmt == nullptr || block_stmt->op != cit_block || block_stmt->cblock == nullptr )
    return false;
  for ( cinsn_t &stmt : *block_stmt->cblock )
    visit(&stmt);
  return true;
}

struct match_orchestration_result_t
{
  cinsn_t *matched_stmt = nullptr;
  xxhash_input_t input;
  const cexpr_t *seed_expr = nullptr;
  const cexpr_t *hash_target = nullptr;
};

// Shared XXH32/XXH64 orchestration: matches the full if/if-else/do-while
// shape rooted at `outer_if` (must have op == cit_if). See
// C:\Users\Ben\.claude\plans\spicy-imagining-church.md for the structural
// description this implements.
bool match_xxhash_pattern(cinsn_t *outer_if, bool is_64, match_orchestration_result_t *out);

} // namespace xxhash_utility

```

`xxhash-utility/src/xxhash_input.cpp`:

```cpp
// MSVC std::string layout constants (x64), duplicated locally from
// IDA-STL-Utility/src/stl_string.cpp rather than depending on that plugin
// being installed -- this plugin must work standalone in a fresh idb.
//   struct basic_string {          // sizeof == 0x20
//     union _Bxty { char _Buf[16]; char *_Ptr; } _Bx;  // offset 0x00
//     size_t _Mysize;                                  // offset 0x10
//     size_t _Myres;                                   // offset 0x18
//   };

#include "xxhash_input.h"
#include "xxhash_common.h"

#include <vector>

namespace xxhash_utility
{

namespace
{

constexpr uint64 BX_PTR_OFFSET = 0x00;
constexpr uint64 MYSIZE_OFFSET = 0x10;
constexpr uint64 MYRES_OFFSET = 0x18;

struct member_hit_t
{
  const cexpr_t *expr;
  member_access_t access;
};

struct member_collector_t : public ctree_visitor_t
{
  std::vector<member_hit_t> hits;
  std::vector<const cexpr_t *> asg_targets;
  std::vector<const cexpr_t *> asg_sources;

  member_collector_t() : ctree_visitor_t(CV_FAST) {}

  int idaapi visit_expr(cexpr_t *e) override
  {
    if ( e->op == cot_memref || e->op == cot_memptr )
    {
      member_access_t access = resolve_member_access(e);
      if ( access.ok )
        hits.push_back({ e, access });
    }
    else if ( e->op == cot_asg )
    {
      asg_targets.push_back(e->x);
      asg_sources.push_back(e->y);
    }
    return 0;
  }
};

} // namespace

bool try_resolve_string_input(cinsn_t *scope, xxhash_input_t *out)
{
  member_collector_t collector;
  collector.apply_to(scope, nullptr);

  const cexpr_t *mysize_expr = nullptr;
  match_identity_t base;
  bool have_myres = false;

  for ( const member_hit_t &hit : collector.hits )
  {
    if ( hit.access.offset == MYSIZE_OFFSET )
    {
      mysize_expr = hit.expr;
      base = hit.access.base;
    }
  }
  if ( mysize_expr == nullptr )
    return false;

  for ( const member_hit_t &hit : collector.hits )
  {
    if ( hit.access.offset == MYRES_OFFSET && hit.access.base == base )
      have_myres = true;
  }
  if ( !have_myres )
    return false;

  // Find a variable that's assigned the base's own address (the small/SSO
  // arm) somewhere in scope -- the pointer-selection variable.
  match_identity_t ptr_var;
  bool have_ptr_var = false;
  for ( size_t i = 0; i < collector.asg_targets.size(); ++i )
  {
    const cexpr_t *source = strip_casts(collector.asg_sources[i]);
    if ( source == nullptr )
      continue;

    bool is_base_address = false;
    if ( source->op == cot_ref )
    {
      const match_identity_t addressed = identify(source->x);
      is_base_address = addressed.valid() && addressed == base;
    }
    else
    {
      const member_access_t access = resolve_member_access(source);
      is_base_address = access.ok && access.offset == BX_PTR_OFFSET && access.base == base;
    }

    if ( is_base_address )
    {
      const match_identity_t target = identify(collector.asg_targets[i]);
      if ( target.valid() )
      {
        ptr_var = target;
        have_ptr_var = true;
        break;
      }
    }
  }
  if ( !have_ptr_var )
    return false;

  // Recover the actual pointer expression to hand back: the assignment
  // target's own expression is fine as a representative reference (it will
  // be cloned before use, per xxhash_synth's contract).
  const cexpr_t *ptr_expr = nullptr;
  for ( size_t i = 0; i < collector.asg_targets.size(); ++i )
  {
    const match_identity_t target = identify(collector.asg_targets[i]);
    if ( target.valid() && target == ptr_var )
    {
      ptr_expr = collector.asg_targets[i];
      break;
    }
  }
  if ( ptr_expr == nullptr )
    return false;

  out->data_ptr = ptr_expr;
  out->length = mysize_expr;
  return true;
}

bool try_resolve_raw_buffer_input(
      const cexpr_t *candidate_ptr,
      const cexpr_t *candidate_length,
      xxhash_input_t *out)
{
  if ( candidate_ptr == nullptr || candidate_length == nullptr )
    return false;

  // Reject if the length expression is actually a std::string's _Mysize
  // member -- that case belongs to try_resolve_string_input instead.
  const member_access_t access = resolve_member_access(candidate_length);
  if ( access.ok && access.offset == MYSIZE_OFFSET )
    return false;

  out->data_ptr = candidate_ptr;
  out->length = candidate_length;
  return true;
}

} // namespace xxhash_utility

```

`xxhash-utility/src/xxhash_input.h`:

```h
#pragma once

#include <hexrays.hpp>

namespace xxhash_utility
{

struct xxhash_input_t
{
  const cexpr_t *data_ptr = nullptr;
  const cexpr_t *length = nullptr;
};

// Recognizes a std::string (MSVC SSO layout) being hashed: looks for
// member accesses to `_Mysize` (offset 0x10) and `_Myres` (offset 0x18)
// sharing the same base object/variable somewhere within `scope`, plus a
// variable that gets assigned either the base's address or its `_Bx._Ptr`
// union member (offset 0x00) -- the SSO-arm pointer selection. On success,
// `out->length` is a `_Mysize` member-access expression and `out->data_ptr`
// is the resolved pointer variable's expression.
bool try_resolve_string_input(cinsn_t *scope, xxhash_input_t *out);

// Fallback for a raw pointer+length buffer: `candidate_ptr`/`candidate_length`
// are expressions already identified by the caller (e.g. the loop's pointer
// operand and the finalization tail's remaining-length operand) -- this
// only rejects the pair if it looks like it's actually the string case
// (so callers should try `try_resolve_string_input` first).
bool try_resolve_raw_buffer_input(
      const cexpr_t *candidate_ptr,
      const cexpr_t *candidate_length,
      xxhash_input_t *out);

} // namespace xxhash_utility

```

`xxhash-utility/src/xxhash_match.h`:

```h
#pragma once

#include "xxhash_common.h"
#include "xxhash_input.h"

namespace xxhash_utility
{

struct xxhash_match_t
{
  cinsn_t *matched_stmt = nullptr; // the outer cit_if to replace
  xxhash_variant_t variant = XXHASH_32;
  xxhash_input_t input;
  const cexpr_t *seed_expr = nullptr;   // 4th arg of the finalize-tail call
  const cexpr_t *hash_target = nullptr; // lvalue the tail call result is assigned to
};

// `outer_if` must have op == cit_if. Returns true and fills `out` if it
// matches the recognized inlined XXH32/XXH64 shape (see
// C:\Users\Ben\.claude\plans\spicy-imagining-church.md for the full
// structural description).
bool try_match_xxh32(cinsn_t *outer_if, xxhash_match_t *out);
bool try_match_xxh64(cinsn_t *outer_if, xxhash_match_t *out);

} // namespace xxhash_utility
```

`xxhash-utility/src/xxhash_plugin.cpp`:

```cpp
// IDA-XXHash-Utility: recognizes compiler-inlined XXH32/XXH64 hashing
// routines in Hex-Rays pseudocode and collapses them into a single
// readable synthetic call. See
// C:\Users\Ben\.claude\plans\spicy-imagining-church.md for the design.

#include "xxhash_match.h"
#include "xxhash_synth.h"

#include <hexrays.hpp>
#include <diskio.hpp>
#include <fpro.h>

#include <cstdarg>
#include <set>

namespace xxhash_utility
{

// Declared in xxhash_common.h; defined here so both this file and
// xxhash_common.cpp (structural-rejection logging) can call it.
void debug_log(const char *format, ...)
{
  qstring path;
  path.sprnt("%s/IDA-XXHash-Utility.log", get_user_idadir());
  FILE *file = qfopen(path.c_str(), "a");
  if ( file == nullptr )
    return;

  qstring message;
  va_list args;
  va_start(args, format);
  message.vsprnt(format, args);
  va_end(args);
  qfprintf(
    file,
    "[%u.%06u] %s\n",
    get_secs(qtime64()),
    get_usecs(qtime64()),
    message.c_str());
  qfclose(file);
}

} // namespace xxhash_utility

namespace
{

using namespace xxhash_utility;

// Runs at hxe_maturity/CMAT_FINAL (before Hex-Rays generates pseudocode
// text), mirroring the ctree-mutation timing of IDA-VTable-Utility's
// vtable_receiver_hider_t -- but here visit_insn (not visit_expr) is
// overridden, since the xxHash shape spans a whole if/if-else/do-while
// statement sequence, not a single expression.
struct xxhash_collapser_t : public ctree_visitor_t
{
  int collapsed_count = 0;

  xxhash_collapser_t() : ctree_visitor_t(CV_FAST) {}

  int idaapi visit_insn(cinsn_t *insn) override
  {
    if ( insn->op != cit_if )
      return 0;

    xxhash_match_t match;
    const char *variant_name = nullptr;
    if ( try_match_xxh32(insn, &match) )
      variant_name = "XXH32";
    else if ( try_match_xxh64(insn, &match) )
      variant_name = "XXH64";
    else
      return 0;

    if ( !synthesize_and_replace(match) )
    {
      debug_log("matched %s pattern at %a but failed to synthesize replacement", variant_name, insn->ea);
      return 0;
    }

    debug_log("collapsed %s pattern at %a", variant_name, insn->ea);
    ++collapsed_count;
    return 0;
  }
};

// Collects the lvar index of every cot_var reference remaining in the
// (already-mutated) ctree.
struct used_var_collector_t : public ctree_visitor_t
{
  std::set<int> used_indices;

  used_var_collector_t() : ctree_visitor_t(CV_FAST) {}

  int idaapi visit_expr(cexpr_t *e) override
  {
    if ( e->op == cot_var )
      used_indices.insert(e->v.idx);
    return 0;
  }
};

// replace_by() deletes the matched statements but leaves the collapsed
// pattern's now-orphaned locals (lane accumulators, the intermediate hash
// variable, the resolved data pointer, etc.) sitting in the function's
// variable list -- Hex-Rays would otherwise still declare them (unused)
// since their CVAR_USED flag was computed before our mutation and is never
// recalculated afterward. Clear it for anything no longer referenced
// anywhere in the final ctree, so only the final hash variable (and any
// other genuinely-still-used locals) remain visible. Function arguments
// are left untouched regardless of use, since hiding those would alter the
// printed function signature, not just a declaration line.
void hide_orphaned_locals(cfunc_t *cfunc)
{
  used_var_collector_t collector;
  collector.apply_to(&cfunc->body, nullptr);

  lvars_t *lvars = cfunc->get_lvars();
  if ( lvars == nullptr )
    return;

  for ( size_t i = 0; i < lvars->size(); ++i )
  {
    lvar_t &lvar = lvars->at(i);
    if ( lvar.is_arg_var() || !lvar.used() )
      continue;
    if ( collector.used_indices.find(int(i)) == collector.used_indices.end() )
      lvar.clear_used();
  }
}

ssize_t idaapi decompiler_callback(void *, hexrays_event_t event, va_list va)
{
  if ( event == hxe_maturity )
  {
    cfunc_t *cfunc = va_arg(va, cfunc_t *);
    const ctree_maturity_t new_maturity = ctree_maturity_t(va_arg(va, int));
    if ( new_maturity == CMAT_FINAL )
    {
      xxhash_collapser_t collapser;
      collapser.apply_to(&cfunc->body, nullptr);
      if ( collapser.collapsed_count > 0 )
      {
        hide_orphaned_locals(cfunc);
        debug_log(
          "function=%a collapsed=%d",
          cfunc->entry_ea,
          collapser.collapsed_count);
      }
    }
    return 0;
  }
  return 0;
}

struct xxhash_plugmod_t final : plugmod_t
{
  xxhash_plugmod_t()
  {
    if ( !install_hexrays_callback(decompiler_callback, this) )
      msg("%s: failed to install the Hex-Rays callback\n", PLUGIN.wanted_name);
    debug_log("plugin initialized: version=0.1.0");
  }

  ~xxhash_plugmod_t() override
  {
    remove_hexrays_callback(decompiler_callback, this);
  }

  bool idaapi run(size_t) override
  {
    return false;
  }
};

plugmod_t *idaapi init()
{
  if ( !init_hexrays_plugin() )
    return nullptr;
  return new xxhash_plugmod_t;
}

} // namespace

plugin_t PLUGIN =
{
  IDP_INTERFACE_VERSION,
  PLUGIN_HIDE | PLUGIN_MULTI,
  init,
  nullptr,
  nullptr,
  "Recognizes compiler-inlined XXH32/XXH64 and collapses them into a readable call.",
  "",
  "IDA-XXHash-Utility",
  ""
};
```

`xxhash-utility/src/xxhash_synth.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "xxhash_synth.h"

#include <typeinf.hpp>

namespace xxhash_utility
{

bool synthesize_and_replace(const xxhash_match_t &match)
{
  if ( match.matched_stmt == nullptr || match.hash_target == nullptr
    || match.input.data_ptr == nullptr || match.input.length == nullptr
    || match.seed_expr == nullptr )
  {
    return false;
  }

  tinfo_t rettype;
  if ( !rettype.create_simple_type(match.variant == XXHASH_64 ? BTF_UINT64 : BTF_UINT32) )
    return false;

  carglist_t *args = new carglist_t();
  args->resize(3);
  args->at(0).consume_cexpr(new cexpr_t(*match.input.data_ptr));
  args->at(1).consume_cexpr(new cexpr_t(*match.input.length));
  args->at(2).consume_cexpr(new cexpr_t(*match.seed_expr));

  cexpr_t *call = call_helper(rettype, args, match.variant == XXHASH_64 ? "XXH64" : "XXH32");
  if ( call == nullptr )
    return false;

  cexpr_t *asg = new cexpr_t();
  asg->op = cot_asg;
  asg->x = new cexpr_t(*match.hash_target);
  asg->y = call;
  asg->type = asg->x->type;
  asg->ea = match.matched_stmt->ea;

  cinsn_t *new_stmt = new cinsn_t();
  new_stmt->op = cit_expr;
  new_stmt->cexpr = asg;
  new_stmt->ea = match.matched_stmt->ea;

  match.matched_stmt->replace_by(new_stmt);
  return true;
}

} // namespace xxhash_utility

```

`xxhash-utility/src/xxhash_synth.h`:

```h
#pragma once

#include "xxhash_match.h"

namespace xxhash_utility
{

// Builds a synthetic `hash_target = XXH32(data_ptr, length, seed);` (or
// XXH64) call statement and splices it in place of `match.matched_stmt`
// via cinsn_t::replace_by. Returns false (without mutating the ctree) if
// any required piece of `match` is missing.
bool synthesize_and_replace(const xxhash_match_t &match);

} // namespace xxhash_utility

```
